So the call site is in AI style 67 (critter AI): if critter center tile has shimmer() liquid > 30, call GetShimmered().

GetShimmered branches:
1. SpawnedFromStatue → ShimmerEffect + deactivate (despawn).
2. ShimmerTransformToNPC[type] >= 0 → Transform to NPC.
3. ShimmerTransformToItem[type] >= 0 → spawn item, deactivate.
4. ShimmerTownTransform[type] → town NPC shimmer (ai[0]=25).

For critters (aiStyle 67), typically they have ShimmerTransformToNPC set to their shimmer variant (e.g. critter → glowing/shimmer critter). 151	 *  （OrderBy(value).First()——等价取组注册序首个,稳定排序）。
152	 *  ⚠️ value 表语义:value JSON 只存 SetDefaults case 内显式赋值;**缺表 = 原版
153	 *  value 0**（Item.ResetStats :48596 的重置默认）,绝非"未知"。曾误用
154	 *  MAX_SAFE_INTEGER 回退 → Wood 组(9 无显式 value=0)被 5215(显式 value)抢位,
155	 *  与原版"木配方 decraft 返还普通木材"相悖 */
156	const GROUP_DECRAFT_ID = new Map<number, number>();
157	export function groupDecraftItemId(groupId: number): number {
158	  const hit = GROUP_DECRAFT_ID.get(groupId);
159	  if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
160	  const items = RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(groupId)] ?? [];
161	  let best = items[0] ?? 0;
162	  let bestV = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
163	  for (const it of items) {
164	    const v = (itemValueJson as Record<string, number>)[String(it)] ?? 0; // 缺表=原版 0(ResetStats 默认)
165	    if (v < bestV) { bestV = v; best = it; }
166	  }
167	  GROUP_DECRAFT_ID.set(groupId, best);
168	  return best;
169	}
170	
171	/** 材料 → decraft 返还物（WorldItem.cs:1895-1897 IsRecipeGroup ? DecraftItemId : id）：
172	 *  材料命中本配方任一 acceptedGroup → 返组代表（groupDecraftItemId） */
173	function decraftItemFor(r: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof recipeAt>>, itemId: number): number {
174	  for (const gid of r.groups) {
175	    const items = RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(gid)];
176	    if (items && (items as number[]).includes(itemId)) return groupDecraftItemId(gid);
177	  }
178	  return itemId;
179	}
180	
181	export interface DecraftPile { vid: number; stack: number }
182	
183	export interface DecraftOutcome {
184	  /** 返还垛（配方材料序；单垛 ≤9999，超额拆垛 = 原版逐垛独立 Item.NewItem :1913-1921） */
185	  piles: DecraftPile[];
186	  /** 原物品消耗量（次数 × createStack，:1927） */
187	  consumed: number;
188	  /** ≥2 材料 → 掉落物按序号抛撒（requiredItem[1].stack > 0，:1882） */
189	  spread: boolean;
190	}
191	
192	/** decraft 返还计算（WorldItem.GetShimmered :1879-1934 纯函数化）：
193	 *  无配方 / 次数 0 → null。rand3 语义 = Main.rand.Next(3)，仅 alchemy 配方消费
194	 *  （逐单位 1/3 概率蒸发，:1904-1911）；注入以便测试确定性断言 */
195	export function decraftOutcome(
196	  vid: number, stack: number,
197	  crimsonWorld: boolean,
198	  rand3: () => number = () => Math.floor(Math.random() * 3),
199	): DecraftOutcome | null {
200	  const idx = getDecraftingRecipeIndex(getShimmerEquivalentType(vid, true), crimsonWorld);
201	  if (idx < 0) return null;
202	  const r = recipeAt(idx)!;
203	  const times = Math.floor(stack / r.createStack);
204	  if (times <= 0) return null;
205	  // AddCustomShimmerResult 覆盖表（:1884-1891）替代材料表
206	  const entries: Array<{ id: number; stack: number }> = r.shimmer?.length
207	    ? r.shimmer.map(([id, st]) => ({ id, stack: st }))
208	    : r.items;
209	  const piles: DecraftPile[] = [];
210	  for (const e of entries) {
211	    if (e.id <= 0 || e.stack <= 0) break;      // :1898-1901 requiredItem 空位终止
212	    let qty = times * e.stack;
213	    if (r.alchemy) {
214	      for (let u = qty; u > 0; u--) if (rand3() === 0) qty--;
215	    }
216	    const target = decraftItemFor(r, e.id);
217	    while (qty > 0) {
218	      const chunk = Math.min(qty, 9999);       // :1913-1916
219	      piles.push({ vid: target, stack: chunk });
220	      qty -= chunk;
221	    }
222	  }
223	  return { piles, consumed: times * r.createStack, spread: (r.items[1]?.stack ?? 0) > 0 };
224	}
225	
226	/** Item.CanShimmer（Item.cs:49045-49068）2026-08-13 补 decraft 位（:49056）：
227	 *  可转 = 未锁 + (有转化目标 ∨ **可分解且未锁** ∨ 钱币)。
228	 *  decraftCtx 缺省 = 不启用 decraft 判定（调用方旧签名兼容） */
229	export function canShimmerItem(
230	  vid: number, downedMoonlord = false, moonPhase?: number,
231	  decraftCtx?: { stack: number; crimsonWorld: boolean; downedBoss3: boolean; downedGolemBoss: boolean },
232	): boolean {
233	  if (isItemTransformLocked(vid, downedMoonlord)) return false;
234	  if (getTransformToItem(vid, moonPhase) > 0 || COMMON_COIN.has(vid)) return true;
235	  if (decraftCtx) return isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked(vid, decraftCtx.stack, decraftCtx.crimsonWorld, decraftCtx.downedBoss3, decraftCtx.downedGolemBoss);
236	  return false;
237	}
238	
239	/** 钱币→微光化币面额放大（WorldItem.cs:1791-1810）：铜 ×1 / 银 ×100 / 金 ×10000 /
240	 *  铂 stack 钳 1 后 ×1000000。返回入 coinLuck 的铜币面额（stack 一并放大） */
241	export function coinLuckAmount(vid: number, stack: number): number {
242	  switch (vid) {
243	    case 72: return stack * 100;
244	    case 73: return stack * 10000;
245	    case 74: return Math.min(stack, 1) * 1000000;
246	    default: return stack; // 71 铜币 ×1
247	  }
248	}
249	
250	// ============ NPC 侧（NPC.GetShimmered，NPC.cs:92502-92584） ============
251	
252	/** NPCID.Sets.ShimmerTransformToNPC（→ NPC.Transform 保持实体） */
253	export function npcShimmerTransformTo(npcId: number): number {
254	  const t = NPC_TRANSFORM[npcId];
255	  return t !== undefined && t >= 0 ? t : -1;
256	}
257	
258	/** NPCID.Sets.ShimmerTransformToItem（→ 掉微光化物品后消散） */
259	export function npcShimmerTransformToItem(npcId: number): number {
260	  const t = NPC_TRANSFORM_ITEM[npcId];
261	  return t !== undefined && t >= 0 ? t : -1;
262	}
263	
264	/** NPCID.Sets.ShimmerTownTransform（城镇 NPC 微光变体，ai[0]=25） */
265	export function isNpcTownShimmerable(npcId: number): boolean {
266	  return NPC_TOWN_TRANSFORM.has(npcId);
267	}
268	
269	// ============ 玩家脱困传送（ShimmerHelper.FindSpotWithoutShimmer 近似） ============
270	
271	/** TryFindingShimmerFreeSpot（Player.cs:28414-28469）净效果版：以 (tx,ty) 为心，
272	 *  半径 r ∈ {1,3,…,59}（原版 num=60 循环步 2）找第一个满足 IsSpotShimmerFree
273	 *  （ShimmerHelper.cs:67-82 三判）的落点：
274	 *  ① 玩家盒 3 格高（y-2..y）无实心无微光（SolidCollision(landing, w, h)）
275	 *  ② 下方 100px（7 格）内有地面（SolidCollision(+height, 100, allowSolidTop)——
276	 *     非紧贴一格；旧实现过严致湖底斜坡/半砖旁找不到）
277	 *  ③ 玩家盒 + 下方 100px 全无微光（WetCollision(landing, w, h+100) && shimmer → 拒）——
278	 *     旧实现漏此判：湖面之上 3 格空气 + 湖面在 7 格内的列被当有效落点 → 传送后
279	 *     落回湖里（用户实测"传送完还在湖里"）
280	 *  **三层兜底**（原版 :28442-28469）：玩家周围失败 → 世界出生点周围 →
281	 *  主出生点（本仓二者同源 spawnX/Y，合并为一层）——保证必能脱困，不会永久卡住 */
282	export function findShimmerFreeSpot(
283	  st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number,
284	  fallbackTx?: number, fallbackTy?: number,
285	): { x: number; y: number } | null {
286	  const clear = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
287	    for (let j = y - 2; j <= y; j++) {
288	      if (!st.inBounds(x, j)) return false;
289	      const i = st.idx(x, j);
290	      if (st.isSolid(x, j) || (st.liquid[i] > 0 && st.liquidType[i] === 4)) return false;
291	    }
292	    return true;
293	  };
294	  // 原版第二判：落点下方 100px（约 7 格）内存在 solid 或 solidTop（allowSolidTop=true）
295	  const groundBelow = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
296	    for (let j = y + 1; j <= y + 7; j++) {
297	      if (!st.inBounds(x, j)) return false;
298	      if (st.isSolid(x, j) || st.isPlatform(x, j)) return true;
299	    }
300	    return false;
301	  };
302	  // 原版第三判（ShimmerHelper.cs:80-81 WetCollision(landing, w, h+100)）：落点
303	  // 下方 100px 内不得有微光——微光列（如湖面正上方的空气）视为无效落点
304	  const shimmerBelow = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
305	    for (let j = y + 1; j <= y + 7; j++) {
306	      if (!st.inBounds(x, j)) return false;
307	      const i = st.idx(x, j);
308	      if (st.liquid[i] > 0 && st.liquidType[i] === 4) return false;
309	    }
310	    return true;
311	  };
312	  const search = (cx: number, cy: number): { x: number; y: number } | null => {
313	    for (let r = 1; r < 60; r += 2) {
314	      // 切比雪夫半径逐圈外扩（原版 i 循环步 2 语义；原版仅扫环上 8 点/圈，
315	      // 此处全量整数扫描为其超集——命中的落点满足同一谓词，行为相容）
316	      for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
317	        for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
318	          if (Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) < r - 1) continue; // 只扫外环
319	          const x = cx + dx, y = cy + dy;
320	          if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !groundBelow(x, y) || !shimmerBelow(x, y)) continue;
321	          if (clear(x, y)) return { x, y };
322	        }
323	      }
324	    }
325	    return null;
326	  };
327	  return search(tx, ty)
328	    ?? (fallbackTx !== undefined && fallbackTy !== undefined ? search(fallbackTx, fallbackTy) : null);
329	}
330	
331	/** 世界坐标落点 → 玩家传送位（左上角 px 锚，ShimmerHelper.cs:9 + Player.cs:28400-28402）。
332	 *  原版 FindSpotWithoutShimmer 返回 vector2 = (tile*16, tile*16) + (-width/2, -height)——
333	 *  即【玩家盒左上角】坐标（脚底正好贴 tile 底边）；ShimmerUnstuck 再 Teleport(vector+(0,-2))。
334	 *  旧实现 {x: tile*16, y: tile*16-2} 漏掉 -width/2/-height → 盒顶对齐格顶、脚部沉入
335	 *  下方实心 40px = 用户实测"半身在地里"。默认 20/42 = Player 构造尺寸（Player.cs:55083）。 */
336	export function shimmerTeleportPos(
337	  spot: { x: number; y: number }, w = 20, h = 42,
338	): { x: number; y: number } {
339	  return { x: spot.x * TILE - w / 2, y: spot.y * TILE - h - 2 };
340	}
341	

```

157	            if (!st.inBounds(ax, 0)) continue;
158	            let y = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
159	            while (y < st.h - 2 && !st.isSolid(ax, y)) y++;
160	            if (!st.inBounds(ax, y - 1)) continue;
161	            if (st.liquid[st.idx(ax, y - 1)] <= 40) { this.facing = dir; this.shoreSeek = 60; return; }
162	          }
163	        }
164	      }
165	    } else if (this.onGround) {
166	      // 地面：15% 低空起飞；前方 5 格内有水则 35% 下水
167	      if (r < 0.15) { this.flying = true; this.vy = -2.6; this.wantLand = false; this.cruiseT = 150 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 150); this.swimLatch = 0; return; }
168	      if (r < 0.50) {
169	        const dir = this.facing;
170	        for (let k = 1; k <= 5; k++) {
171	          const ax = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE) + dir * k;
172	          if (st.inBounds(ax, 0) && st.liquid[st.idx(ax, Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))] > 80) {
173	            this.shoreSeek = 0; // 保持朝向走进水里
174	            return;
175	          }
176	        }
177	      }
178	    }
179	  }
180	
181	  /** 地面 AI：闲逛跳行/爬行，玩家靠近逃离 */
182	  private groundAI(game: GameHooks, flee: boolean, player: Player | null) {
183	    const inWater = this.inWater; // fixedUpdate 已刷新
184	
185	    if (flee && player) this.facing = player.cx < this.cx ? 1 : -1;
186	    else if (this.shoreSeek > 0) {
187	      // 上岸中：保持锁定方向（到岸后由 duckIdle/换向逻辑解除）
188	      this.shoreSeek--;
189	      if (!this.inWater) this.shoreSeek = 0;
190	    } else if (--this.wanderT <= 0) {
191	      this.wanderT = 90 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 150);
192	      if (Math.random() < 0.5) this.facing = -this.facing;
193	    }
194	
195	    if (this.def.kind === 'hop' && this.def.water && !inWater) {
196	      // 两栖种（鸭子）岸上：持续踱步（原版鸭子是走路不是蹦），
197	      // 偶尔小跳——只在水里才用跳行推进
198	      this.vx = this.facing * this.def.speed * (flee ? 1.8 : 0.8);
199	      if (--this.hopCd <= 0 && this.onGround) {
200	        this.hopCd = 90 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 120);
201	        this.vy = -(2.2 + Math.random() * 0.6); // 偶尔轻跳一下
202	      }
203	    } else if (this.def.kind === 'hop') {
204	      // 跳行：静止蓄力 → 起跳（逃离时高频远跳）
205	      this.vx *= 0.8;
206	      if (--this.hopCd <= 0 && this.onGround) {
207	        this.hopCd = flee ? 18 : 50 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 60);
208	        this.vx = this.facing * this.def.speed * (flee ? 1.6 : 1);
209	        this.vy = -(3.2 + Math.random() * 0.8);
210	      }
211	    } else {
212	      this.vx = this.facing * this.def.speed * (flee ? 2.2 : 1);
213	    }
214	
215	    // 可游泳生物（鸭子）：平稳浮水——小浮力 + 强阻尼，
216	    // 出水瞬间的微下落由滞回期游水帧遮盖，不再大幅上下弹跳。
217	    // 上岸推进：游泳中 onGround 恒 false、hop 不触发 → shoreSeek 时直接给水平游速
218	    if (this.def.water && inWater) {
219	      this.vy = (this.vy - 0.14) * 0.82;
220	      if (this.shoreSeek > 0) this.vx = this.facing * this.def.speed;
221	      else this.vx *= 0.94;
222	      moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
223	      return;
224	    }
225	    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
226	    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
227	    if (this.hitWall && this.onGround) this.facing = -this.facing;
228	  }
229	
230	  /** 飞行 AI：绕巡航点飘，玩家靠近飞离 */
231	  private flyAI(game: GameHooks, flee: boolean, player: Player | null) {
232	    this.flyMove(game, flee, player, false);
233	  }
234	
235	  /** 飞行移动核心。landing=true 时关闭悬停改用重力下落找地面 */
236	  private flyMove(game: GameHooks, flee: boolean, player: Player | null, landing: boolean) {
237	    const steer = 0.09;
238	    if (flee && player) {
239	      const dx = this.cx - player.cx, dy = this.cy - player.cy;
240	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
241	      this.vx += (dx / d) * steer * 3;
242	      this.vy += (dy / d) * steer * 3;
243	      this.facing = dx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
244	    } else if (this.def.perch || this.def.fleeFly) {
245	      // 鸟/鸭：持久航向直线巡航，每 2-5 秒小角度改向（±35°），飞出画面自然销毁
246	      if (--this.headingT <= 0) {
247	        this.headingT = 120 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 200);
248	        this.heading += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2;
249	      }
250	      this.vx += Math.cos(this.heading) * steer;
251	      this.vy += Math.sin(this.heading) * steer * 0.4;
252	      this.facing = this.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
253	    } else {
254	      // 蝴蝶/萤火虫：小范围低空飘（不远离出生地太多）
255	      if (--this.wanderT <= 0) {
256	        this.wanderT = 50 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 100);
257	        this.target = {
258	          x: this.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 130,
259	          y: this.cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60,
260	        };
261	      }
262	      const t = this.target ?? { x: this.cx, y: this.cy };
263	      const dx = t.x - this.cx, dy = t.y - this.cy;
264	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
265	      this.vx += (dx / d) * steer;
266	      this.vy += (dy / d) * steer;
267	      this.facing = dx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
268	    }
269	    this.vx *= 0.96; this.vy *= 0.96;
270	    const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
271	    const max = this.def.speed * (flee ? 2 : 1);
272	    if (spd > max) { this.vx = (this.vx / spd) * max; this.vy = (this.vy / spd) * max; }
273	    if (landing) {
274	      // 降落模式：重力下落 + 保留水平漂移，落地由外层接管（有意落水不避）
275	      this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY * 0.5, MAX_FALL_SPEED * 0.6);
276	      moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
277	      return;
278	    }
279	    // 通用飞行避水（def.avoidWater）：巡航/逃离中不扎进水里；鸭子保航向爬升
280	    if (this.def.avoidWater && avoidWater(this, game.world, !this.def.fleeFly)) return;
281	    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
282	    if (this.hitWall) this.vx = -this.vx * 0.6;
283	    if (this.hitHead) this.vy = -Math.abs(this.vy) * 0.6;
284	    if (this.onGround) this.vy = -Math.abs(this.vy) - 0.4;
285	  }
286	
287	  /** 被击中即死：红色血粒子 + 死亡音效（原版小动物死亡走通用 NPC_Killed 槽） */
288	  hurt(game: GameHooks): boolean {
289	    if (this.iframes > 0) return false;
290	    this.dead = true;
291	    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#C03030', 6, 1.8);
292	    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#8A1818', 4, 1.2);
293	    // 小动物死亡声：与史莱姆一致（NPC_Killed_1）；
294	    // 主角击杀与怪物碾压都走这里，两条路径音效完全一致
295	    game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Killed_1'], 0.8);
296	    return true;
297	  }
298	
299	  draw() { /* Renderer 统一绘制 */ }
300	}
301	

```

56	      const GRAB_RANGE = 42 + ((player as { grabRangeBonus?: number }).grabRangeBonus ?? 0)
57	        // 拾心药水（lifeMagnet）：红心单独扩大拾取范围
58	        + (this.pickup === 'heart' ? ((player as { heartGrabBonus?: number }).heartGrabBonus ?? 0) : 0)
59	        // 魔力磁石（manaMagnet：星 184/1735/1868 专用吸附，Player.cs:34495 专属拉取通道）
60	        + (this.pickup === 'star' ? ((player as { manaMagnetBonus?: number }).manaMagnetBonus ?? 0) : 0);
61	      const touching =
62	        this.x < player.x + player.w && this.x + this.w > player.x &&
63	        this.y < player.y + player.h && this.y + this.h > player.y;
64	      const inGrabRange =
65	        this.x < player.x + player.w + GRAB_RANGE && this.x + this.w > player.x - GRAB_RANGE &&
66	        this.y < player.y + player.h + GRAB_RANGE && this.y + this.h > player.y - GRAB_RANGE;
67	      // 微光化物品上浮中不可拾（GrabItems Player.cs:34466：shimmered 且速度 ≥0.2 跳过）
68	      const grabbable = !(this.shimmered && Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) >= 0.2);
69	      if (touching && grabbable) {
70	        if (this.pickup) {
71	          if (this.pickup === 'heart') {
72	            player.hp = Math.min(player.maxHp, player.hp + 20);
73	            game.addDamageNumber(player.cx, player.y, 20, false, '#40E080');
74	          } else {
75	            player.mana = Math.min(player.maxMana, player.mana + 100);
76	            game.addDamageNumber(player.cx, player.y, 100, false, '#4080FF');
77	          }
78	          this.dead = true;
79	          game.playSfx('pickup', 0.75); // 原版 SoundID 7 Grab
80	          return;
81	        }
82	        const before = this.stack;
83	        const left = player.inv.add(this.itemId, this.stack);
84	        if (left === 0) {
85	          this.dead = true;
86	          game.notifyInventoryChanged();
87	          // 钱币拾取用专属音效，其余走通用拾取（vi_71-74 单轨）
88	          // 音量略低（0.75）：拾取与挖掘声同时触发时两者都可闻，不被 Grab 盖住
89	          game.playSfx(coinIndexOf(this.itemId) >= 0 ? 'coin' : 'pickup', 0.75);
90	          const def = ITEM_DEFS[this.itemId];
91	          if (def) game.showPickupLabel(def.key);
92	          return;
93	        }
94	        if (left !== before) game.notifyInventoryChanged();
95	        this.stack = left;
96	      } else if (inGrabRange) {
97	        // PullItem_Common(:34533-34584):每轴 0.45 步进、钳 4(水平含 player.vx),
98	        // 速度反向时附加 ×0.75 反拉——被拉取帧跳过瓦片碰撞(WorldItem.cs:587-597
99	        // else 分支仅 position+=velocity),物品穿墙飞向玩家
100	        beingGrabbed = true;
101	        const SPEED = 0.45, MAXV = 4, BACK = 0.75;
102	        if (player.cx > this.cx) {
103	          if (this.vx < MAXV + player.vx) this.vx += SPEED;
104	          if (this.vx < 0) this.vx += SPEED * BACK;
105	        } else {
106	          if (this.vx > -MAXV + player.vx) this.vx -= SPEED;
107	          if (this.vx > 0) this.vx -= SPEED * BACK;
108	        }
109	        if (player.cy > this.cy) {
110	          if (this.vy < MAXV) this.vy += SPEED;
111	          if (this.vy < 0) this.vy += SPEED * BACK;
112	        } else {
113	          if (this.vy > -MAXV) this.vy -= SPEED;
114	          if (this.vy > 0) this.vy -= SPEED * BACK;
115	        }
116	      }
117	    }
118	
119	    if (beingGrabbed) {
120	      // 原版被抓取帧:无重力无碰撞,直接位移(WorldItem.cs:596 ApplyMovement)
121	      this.x += this.vx;
122	      this.y += this.vy;
123	    } else {
124	      // 液体采样（身体中心格；本仓液体编码 1水 2岩浆 3蜂蜜 4微光）
125	      const st0 = game.world.store;
126	      const ci = st0.idx(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE));
127	      this.shimmerWet = st0.liquidType[ci] === 4 && st0.liquid[ci] > 30;
128	      if (this.shimmered) {
129	        // 已微光化：无重力上浮（:486-511 gravity=0，vy -0.05/t 钳 -4）
130	        this.vy = Math.max(this.vy - 0.05, -4);
131	        this.vx *= 0.95;
132	      } else {
133	        // 微光浸入重力（:434-441：0.065 / maxFall 4，比水 0.08/5 更缓）
134	        const g = this.shimmerWet ? GRAVITY * 0.7 * (0.065 / 0.3) : GRAVITY * 0.7;
135	        const mf = this.shimmerWet ? 4 : MAX_FALL_SPEED;
136	        this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + g, mf);
137	        this.vx *= 0.96;
138	      }
139	      moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
140	    }
141	    // 入水小水花声（掉落物轻，音量小）。wet 判定排除岩浆/微光（Collision.cs:1418
142	    // WetCollision 只认 !lava && !shimmer）；微光入池走专属 Shimmer 音（:19 style 4）
143	    const st = game.world.store;
144	    const li = st.liquid[st.idx(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))];
145	    const liqType = st.liquidType[st.idx(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))];
146	    const wasWater = this.inWater;
147	    this.inWater = li > 80 && (liqType === 1 || liqType === 3);
148	    if (this.inWater && !wasWater && this.vy > 1.2) game.playSfx('splash', 0.35);
149	    if (this.shimmerWet && !this.shimmered) this.updateShimmer(game);
150	    void ITEM_DEFS;
151	  }
152	
153	  /** WorldItem.Shimmering（:806-840）：脚上方格是微光且可转化 → 进度 +0.01/t，
154	   *  >0.9 触发 GetShimmered；不在微光中则进度衰减。转化触发在本地玩家侧语义
155	   *  （netMode!=1 门对单人恒真） */
156	  private updateShimmer(game: GameHooks): void {
157	    const st = game.world.store;
158	    const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE);
159	    // :810-812 采样 (Center.X/16, position.Y/16 - 1)——物品顶上一格
160	    const ty = Math.floor(this.y / TILE) - 1;
161	    const vid = vanillaIdOfItem(this.itemId);
162	    const inShimmer = st.inBounds(tx, ty)
163	      && st.liquid[st.idx(tx, ty)] > 0 && st.liquidType[st.idx(tx, ty)] === 4;
164	    const flags = (game.world as unknown as { flags: Record<string, boolean> }).flags ?? {};
165	    // :49056 decraft 位:有配方可分解的物品也进入微光(原先只有转化目标才进)
166	    const decraftCtx = {
167	      stack: this.stack,
168	      crimsonWorld: !!game.world.crimson,
169	      downedBoss3: !!(flags['downed_35'] || flags.downedSkeletron),
170	      downedGolemBoss: !!flags['downed_245'],
171	    };
172	    // 月后锁取世界 flags（原版 NPC.downedMoonlord；恒 false 会把月后物永久挡在微光外）
173	    const downedMoonlord = !!flags['downed_398'];
174	    if (inShimmer && canShimmerItem(vid, downedMoonlord, game.world.clock.moonPhase, decraftCtx)) {
175	      this.shimmerTime += 0.01;
176	      if (this.shimmerTime > 0.9) {
177	        this.shimmerTime = 0.9;
178	        this.getShimmered(game);
179	      }
180	    } else if (this.shimmerTime > 0) {
181	      this.shimmerTime = Math.max(0, this.shimmerTime - 0.01);
182	    }
183	  }
184	
185	  /** WorldItem.GetShimmered（:1781-1931）分支顺序 1:1：钱币(:1790) →
186	   *  ShimmerTransformToItem(:1812) → decraft(:1878) → makeNPC/特例 4986、560
187	   *  登记未移植（Shimmer.ts GAP 清单）。**decraft 必须排在钱币/转化之后**：
188	   *  钱币 71-74 与火炬 8 等都有 IsCrafted 配方，放前面会抢走转化/coinLuck 分支 */
189	  private getShimmered(game: GameHooks): void {
190	    const vid = vanillaIdOfItem(this.itemId);
191	    if (vid === 71 || vid === 72 || vid === 73 || vid === 74) {
192	      // 钱币 → 微光化币（:1791-1810）：面额放大后 AddCoinLuck（距离 <1000px 才累计，
193	      // LuckState.addCoinLuck 1:1），物品消失
194	      const amount = coinLuckAmount(vid, this.stack);
195	      const player = (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player;
196	      const dist = Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy);
197	      player.luckState.addCoinLuck(dist, amount);
198	      this.stack = 0;
199	      this.dead = true;
200	      return;
201	    }
202	    const target = getTransformToItem(vid, game.world.clock.moonPhase);   // 3461 月相砖动态分支
203	    if (target > 0) {
204	      const iid = internalIdOfVanilla(target);
205	      if (iid >= 0) {
206	        this.itemId = iid;       // SetDefaults(target) 原堆叠保留（:1813-1817）
207	        this.shimmered = true;    // 上浮漂浮
208	        this.shimmerTime = 1;
209	        this.vx *= 0.1; this.vy *= 0.1;
210	        game.playSfx('shimmerSplash', 0.75, this.cx, this.cy);
211	        return;
212	      }
213	    }
214	    this.tryDecraft(game, vid);   // :1878 else-if 链末端
215	  }
216	
217	  /** WorldItem.GetShimmered decraft 分支（:1878-1946）1:1：
218	   *  纯计算走 Shimmer.decraftOutcome（材料序返还；组材料【替换】为组代表
219	   *  RecipeGroup.DecraftItemId——非追加；customShimmerResults 覆盖表；炼金逐单位
220	   *  1/3 蒸发；单垛 ≤9999）。此处仅负责逐垛 spawn（shimmered 上浮 + ≥2 材料
221	   *  按序号散射）与扣减 */
222	  private tryDecraft(game: GameHooks, vid: number): boolean {
223	    const flags = (game.world as unknown as { flags: Record<string, boolean> }).flags ?? {};
224	    const crimsonWorld = !!game.world.crimson;
225	    const downedBoss3 = !!(flags['downed_35'] || flags.downedSkeletron);
226	    const downedGolemBoss = !!flags['downed_245'];
227	    // :1878 入口 = IsItemDecraftableAndIsDecraftUnlocked（配方存在 ∧ 未锁 ∧ ≥1 次）
228	    if (!isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked(vid, this.stack, crimsonWorld, downedBoss3, downedGolemBoss)) {
229	      return false;
230	    }
231	    const outcome = decraftOutcome(vid, this.stack, crimsonWorld);
232	    if (!outcome) return false;   // 空垛不回退：炼金全蒸发时原版照扣 stack（:1879-1933）
233	    outcome.piles.forEach((p, k) => {
234	      const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[p.vid];
235	      if (!key) return;
236	      const n = k + 1;   // 原版 num7 自 1 起（:1881/1929-1936）
237	      let vx = this.vx * 0.1;
238	      if (outcome.spread) vx = n * (1 + n * 0.05) * (n % 2 === 0 ? -1 : 1);
239	      const d = game.spawnDrop(this.cx, this.cy, key, p.stack, vx, this.vy * 0.1);
240	      if (!d) return;
241	      d.shimmered = true;   // :1924 上浮漂浮
242	      d.shimmerTime = 1;    // :1923
243	    });
244	    this.stack -= outcome.consumed;              // :1927 stack -= 次数×createStack
245	    if (this.stack <= 0) { this.stack = 0; this.dead = true; }
246	    else this.shimmerTime = 1;                   // :1936 剩余 stack 保留并重置浸入进度
247	    game.playSfx('shimmerSplash', 0.75, this.cx, this.cy);
248	    return true;
249	  }
250	
251	  inWater = false;
252	
253	  draw() { /* Renderer 统一绘制 */ }
254	}
255

// 小动物（无害生物）定义：原版 NPC 贴图表驱动
// kind: hop=跳跃移动 / walk=爬行 / fly=飞行；素材为纵向帧条
export type CritterKind = 'hop' | 'walk' | 'fly';

export interface CritterDef {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  npc: number;            // NPC_N 贴图表 id
  kind: CritterKind;
  w: number; h: number;   // 碰撞盒 px
  speed: number;          // 移动速度
  fleeRange: number;      // 逃离玩家的距离（px）
  weight: number;         // 生成权重
  dayOnly?: boolean;
  nightOnly?: boolean;
  gold?: boolean;         // 金色变种（稀有）
  water?: boolean;        // 可游泳（鸭子）
  glow?: boolean;         // 自发光（萤火虫）
  avoidWater?: boolean;   // 飞行避水（鸭子/鸟：巡航中不扎进水里）
  perch?: boolean;        // 飞行种会落地栖息（鸟）
  oceanOnly?: boolean;   // 海洋专属（螃蟹/海鸥：仅地图边缘海洋带，不进沙漠）
  fleeFly?: boolean;      // 地面种受惊会起飞逃离（鸭子）
  /** 状态动画分段 [起始帧, 帧数]（缺省=全表循环） */

// 原版 1.4.0.5 全量 NPC 数据（tools/extract-npcs.mjs 从 Terarria1405/NPC.cs SetDefaults 提取）。
// 增量补齐（2026-08-11）：473-476/590/591/594/628/629/631/634/635/692 十三条取自
// Terarria1456/NPC.cs SetDefaults + Main.cs npcFrameCount（数值一律取 1456 最终态）。
// 弹跳球族增量（2026-08-11）：665 ChaosBallTim / 666 VileSpitEaterOfWorlds 两条取自
// Terarria1456/NPC.cs SetDefaults :17338-17367（aiStyle 9，字段对齐 30/112 同族；贴图
// NPC_665/666.png 已在 sprites，此前纯数据缺口）。
// 星璇塔增量（2026-08-11）：403/404（星尘蠕虫身/尾，字段复制 402 改宽高——1456 无独立
// SetDefaults，原版由 DrawNPC case 402 自绘 trail；本仓蠕虫链 spawnWormChain 需要
// 头+1/头+2 条目）、408（小水流入侵者，复制 407 缩小——1456 无独立 SetDefaults）。
// aiStyle 与 AI_XXX 方法的对应见 NPC.cs AI() 分发；此处先覆盖高频家族，
// 未移植 AI 家族的 aiStyle 回退到最接近的已移植行为（注释标注）。
import vanillaNpcsJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla-npcs.json';
import vanillaDataJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla.json';
import { ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';
import { TILE_DEFS } from './tiles';

/** tile id → key 反查（biomeAt 群系判定用） */
const TILE_KEY_NAME: string[] = TILE_DEFS.map((d) => d.key);

// （旧掉落表已退役：掉落走 src/drops/NpcDrops.ts 规则树——
//  tools/extract-npcdrops.mjs 结构化提取 ItemDropDatabase.cs，含专家/大师/条件/链语义）

/** 原版物品 id → 本仓库 item key（vanilla.json 的 key 是 PascalCase，ITEM_BY_KEY 多为 snake_case；
 *  未注册的返回 null 跳过） */
export const vanillaItemKey = (() => {
  const map = new Map<number, string | null>();
  const items = (vanillaDataJson as unknown as { items: Record<string, { key?: string }> }).items ?? {};
  return (itemId: number): string | null => {
    if (map.has(itemId)) return map.get(itemId)!;
    const meta = items[String(itemId)];
    let key: string | null = null;
    if (meta?.key) {
      const snake = meta.key.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2').toLowerCase();
      if (ITEM_BY_KEY[snake] != null) key = snake;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[meta.key] != null) key = meta.key;
      // vi_NNN 导入物品（items.ts 的全量原版物品命名）：vi_<id> 或 vi_<id>_<snake>
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}`;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}_${snake}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}_${snake}`;
    }
    map.set(itemId, key);
    return key;
  };
})();

export interface VanillaNpc {

21	/** 取 `public static T[] NAME = Factory.CreateIntSet(args...);` 的实参数组 */
22	function intSetArgs(src, name) {
23	  const m = src.match(new RegExp(`public static int\\[\\] ${name} = Factory\\.CreateIntSet\\(([\\s\\S]*?)\\);`));
24	  if (!m) throw new Error(`int set ${name} not found`);
25	  return m[1].split(',').map((s) => parseInt(s.trim(), 10)).filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n));
26	}
27	
28	/** CreateIntSet(default, k, v, ...) → { from: to }（保留 ≤0 值的显式覆盖项） */
29	function intSetMap(src, name) {
30	  const args = intSetArgs(src, name);
31	  const def = args[0];
32	  const out = {};
33	  for (let i = 1; i + 1 < args.length + 1; i += 2) {
34	    if (i + 1 >= args.length + 1) break;
35	    const k = args[i], v = args[i + 1];
36	    if (k === undefined || v === undefined) break;
37	    if (v === def) continue;
38	    out[k] = v;
39	  }
40	  return out;
41	}
42	
43	/** 取 `public static bool[] NAME = Factory.CreateBoolSet(args...);` 成员表 */
44	function boolSetList(src, name) {
45	  const m = src.match(new RegExp(`public static bool\\[\\] ${name} = Factory\\.CreateBoolSet\\(([\\s\\S]*?)\\);`));
46	  if (!m) throw new Error(`bool set ${name} not found`);
47	  const args = m[1].split(',').map((s) => parseInt(s.trim(), 10)).filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n));
48	  // CreateBoolSet 可带第二参 default（CreateBoolSet(true, ids...) = 反选表）——
49	  // 本表全部为默认 false 的正向成员表，args 全为 id。
50	  return args;
51	}
52

// 实体与 Game 交互的接口（避免循环依赖，只放类型）
import type { World } from '../world/World';

export interface GameHooks {
  world: World;
  /** 主角(投射物命中判定用) */
  player: import('./Player').Player;
  /** 敌人列表(投射物命中判定用) */
  enemies(): unknown[];
  /** 城镇 NPC 自卫弹入投射物桶(attackUpdate 消费) */
  spawnTownShot?(shot: import('./TownShot').TownShot): void;
  /** 小动物列表(弹幕命中判定用;近战挥砍在 Game 内直接遍历桶) */
  critters(): unknown[];
  /** 在世界上生成掉落物（返回实体便于染色等后处理） */
  spawnDrop(x: number, y: number, itemKey: string, count?: number, vx?: number, vy?: number): import('./ItemDrop').ItemDrop | null;
  /** 对玩家造成伤害（attacker 用于荆棘反伤） */
  damagePlayer(dmg: number, srcX: number, srcY: number, attacker?: unknown): void;
  /** 伤害数字飘字 */
  addDamageNumber(x: number, y: number, amount: number, crit: boolean, color?: string): void;
  /** 弹幕砍 tileCut 物（Projectile.CutTiles 语义：箭扫过杂草/瓦罐 → Game 内部分流） */
  cutTile(x: number, y: number): void;
  /** 传送门弹 601 落门（PortalHelper.TryPlacingPortal + AddPortal；Game 实现入 props 桶） */
  placePortal?(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, form: number): void;
  /** 破坏 tile（掉落+音效+帧刷新；Enemy 攻门拆门 = WorldGen.KillTile 语义） */
  breakTile?(x: number, y: number): void;
  /** 敌人死亡 */
  onEnemyKilled(key: string, enemy?: import('../entities/Enemy').Enemy): void;
  /** 出生敌人 */
  spawnEnemy(key: string, x: number, y: number): void;
  /** 粒子 */
  spawnParticles(
    x: number, y: number, color: string, count: number, speed?: number,
    opts?: { life?: number; damp?: number; grav?: number; size?: number },
  ): void;
  /** 定向粒子爆发（烟花爆花/喷泉/马桶水花等原版 dust 带初速方向的场景；
   *  spawnParticles 为全向均匀发射，无法表达定向）。dir 为中心角、spread 为半散布，
   *  xMul/yMul 为原版轴向压缩（如 168 尘 y×0.75、马桶尘 x×0.5） */
  spawnBurst?(
    x: number, y: number, color: string, count: number, speed: number,
    dir: number, spread: number,
    opts?: { life?: number; damp?: number; grav?: number; xMul?: number; yMul?: number },
  ): void;
  /** 拾取物品后刷新 UI（掉落物实体调用） */
  notifyInventoryChanged(): void;
  /** 弹幕消亡钩子（Projectile.Kill 按弹型分发；x/y/w/h = 弹体命中盒） */
  onProjectileDeath?(projId: number, x: number, y: number, w?: number, h?: number): void;
  /** 粉末云实效（净化粉 proj 10 / 邪恶粉 11 / 猩红粉 463，Projectile.cs:22399-22533
   *  AI_006 每帧回调）：命中盒内逐格 WorldGen.Convert + Damage_TryUsingPowders（:13646）
   *  NPC 转化。Game 实现（evolution.convert + bound 税务官 Transform 链） */
  applyPowder?(projId: number, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number): void;
  /** 吸血链（Projectile.vampireHeal/ghostHeal）：返回实际回复 HP（0 = 未生效） */
  applyLifeSteal?(kind: 'vampire' | 'ghost', dmg: number, numHits: number, x: number, y: number, isMagic?: boolean): number;
  /** 播放音效 */
  // 世界坐标声（原版 SoundEngine.PlaySound 带 x/y）：x/y 省略 = UI/玩家动作声不衰减
  playSfx(name: string, vol?: number, x?: number, y?: number): void;
  playSfxFiles(files: string[], vol?: number, x?: number, y?: number): void; // 怪物专属声效（原版文件名列表）
  /** 矿车碾过压板轨道（Minecart.HitTrackSwitch → Wiring.HitSwitch，Minecart.cs:1287-1294）。

  /** 按原版文件名直播（无合成兜底；缓冲未就位时仅触发后台懒加载，本播静默） */

/** 小动物入微光（NPC.cs:34586-34598 + GetShimmered:92502-92533）：
 *  触发条件比怪物侧严——中心格(Center)微光液面 >30 即刻转化（无 90t 浸入进度，
 *  aiStyle 67 分支无 shimmerTransparency 累积）。表内小动物（兔/鸟/松鼠/蝴蝶/
 *  金系…）→ 677 城镇史莱姆（NPCID.cs:4843）；螃蟹 67 无表项 → 原版分支链落空，
 *  小动物原地保留（不消散）。 */
private tryShimmer(game: GameHooks): boolean {
  const st = game.world.store;
  const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE);
  const ty = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
  if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) return false;
  const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
  if (!(st.liquidType[i] === 4 && st.liquid[i] > 30)) return false;
  const to = npcShimmerTransformTo(this.def.npc);
  if (to < 0) return false;   // :92522 无表项：分支链落空，不消散
  this.dead = true;
  const e = game.spawnNpcByVanilla?.(to, this.cx, this.y + this.h);
  if (e) {
    // NPC.Transform(:81850)：newType SetDefaults 后贴底重定位；微光化新生渐显
    e.spawnAlpha = 254; e.alphaFade = true;
  }
  game.playSfx('shimmerSplash', 0.75, this.cx, this.cy);
  return true;
}
```

Wait: does Enemy have public spawnAlpha/alphaFade? From fromVanilla: `e.spawnAlpha`, `e.alphaFade` — yes fields exist.

Hmm, does 'shimmerSplash' sfx exist? ItemDrop uses game.playSfx('shimmerSplash', ...). Yes.

 "new_string": "const itemid = read('Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/ItemID.cs');\nconst npcid = read('Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/NPCID.cs');\nconst itemcs = read('Terarria1456/Terraria/Item.cs');\n\n/** Item.cs SetDefaults 内 `case N:` → DefaultToCapturedCritter(X) 扫描。\n *  只收 SetDefaults 顶层 case（2 个 tab 缩进）——内层 switch 的 case 更深，\n *  避免多标签 fallthrough（如 1993/2001 共体）之外的内层标签污染。\n *  多标签共享体（case A: case B: 同一 DefaultToCapturedCritter）逐标签登记 */\nfunction capturedCritterMap(src) {\n  const out = {};\n  const lines = src.split('\\n');\n  let pending = [];\n  let sawSwitch = false;\n  for (const line of lines) {\n    if (/^\tpublic void SetDefaults\\(/.test(line) || /^\tprivate void SetDefaults\\(/.test(line)) sawSwitch = true;\n    if (/^\\t\\tcase (\\d+):\\s*$/.test(line)) {\n      pending.push(parseInt(RegExp.$1, 10));\n      continue;\n    }\n    const m = line.match(/^\\t\\t\\tDefaultToCapturedCritter\\((\\d+)\\);/);\n    if (m && pending.length) {\n      for (const id of pending) out[id] = parseInt(m[1], 10);\n      pending = [];\n      continue;\n    }\n    // 块结束（break/return/goto）清空待定标签\n    if (/^\\t\\t\\t(break|return|goto )/.test(line)) pending = [];\n    // 深层标签（内层 switch 体）不记入 pending 但也不能穿透到外层——\n    // DefaultToCapturedCritter 只出现在 3 tab 深度，安全\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nconst data = {\n  itemTransformTo: intSetMap(itemid, 'ShimmerTransformToItem'),\n  itemCountsAs: intSetMap(itemid, 'ShimmerCountsAsItem'),\n  itemCountsAsDecraft: intSetMap(itemid, 'ShimmerCountsAsItemForDecraft'),\n  itemPostMoonlord: boolSetList(itemid, 'ShimmerPostMoonlord'),\n  commonCoin: boolSetList(itemid, 'CommonCoin'),\n  itemMakeNPC: capturedCritterMap(itemcs),\n  npcTransformTo: intSetMap(npcid, 'ShimmerTransformToNPC'),\n  npcTransformToItem: intSetMap(npcid, 'ShimmerTransformToItem'),\n  npcTownTransform: boolSetList(npcid, 'ShimmerTownTransform'),\n};"
}

[''])[0].length;\n      groups = groups.filter((g) => g.depth < d - 1);\n      continue;\n    }\n    const m = line.match(/^\\\\t+DefaultToCapturedCritter\\\\((.+)\\\\);$/);\n    if (m) {\n      const g = groups[groups.length - 1];\n      if (g) for (const id of g.ids) out[id] = evalCritterArg(m[1], id);\n    }\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** DefaultToCapturedCritter 实参求值：字面量或含 `type`（=物品 id）的算术式 */\nfunction evalCritterArg(arg, itemId) {\n  const wrap = arg.match(/^\\\\(short\\\\)\\\\((.+)\\\\)$/);\n  const expr = wrap ? wrap[1] : arg;\n  if (!/^[\\\\d+\\\\-*/(). type]+$/.test(expr)) {\n    throw new Error(`unparsed DefaultToCapturedCritter arg: ${arg}`);\n  }\n  // eslint-disable-next-line no-new-func\n  return new Function('type', `\"use strict\";return (${expr});`)(itemId);\n}'''\nout = lines[:58] + new_block.split('\\n') + lines[86:]\nopen(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\\n'.join(out))\nprint('replaced; total lines now', len(out))\nEOF\nnode tools/extract-shimmer.mjs && python3 -c \"\nimport json\na = json.load(open('src/data/vanilla-shimmer.json'))\nb = json.load(open('/tmp/vanilla-shimmer.before.json'))\nfor k in b:\n    if k in a and b[k] != a[k]: print('DIFF', k)\nm = a['itemMakeNPC']\nprint('itemMakeNPC count:', len(m))\nfor k in ['2205','2673','2889','2890','2895','4334','4339','4831','4837','4838','4844','261','1338','1992','1994','2001']:\n    print(k, '->', m.get(k))\nprint('26xx region:', {k:v for k,v in m.items() if k.startswith('26')})\n\"",

```js
function evalPrice(expr) {
  // normalize
  expr = expr.trim();
  // handle (int)((double)expr * 1.5)
  // replace price calls with numbers first
  const priceRe = /(?:sellPrice|buyPrice)\(([^()]*)\)/g;
  let out = expr.replace(priceRe, (_, args) => {
    const a = args.split(',').map(s => parseInt(s.trim(), 10) || 0);
    return String(a[0]*1000000 + a[1]*10000 + a[2]*100 + (a[3]||0));
  });
  // strip (int)/(double)/(float) casts
  out = out.replace(/\((?:int|double|float)\)\s*/g, '');
  // remaining must be simple arithmetic with integer literals
  if (!/^[\d+\-*/(). ]+$/.test(out)) return null;
  try { return Math.trunc(new Function(`"use strict";return (${out});`)()); } catch { return null; }
}
```
Careful: `(double)sellPrice(0, 7) * 1.5` → after replace: `(double)70000 * 1.5` → strip cast → `70000 * 1.5` = 105000 ✓. `buyPrice(0, 5) + sellPrice(0, 0, 3)` → `50000 + 300` ✓.

[]).length - (LINES[i].match(/}/g) ?? []).length;\n    if (depth === 0 && i > sig) return [sig, i];\n  }\n  throw new Error(`method ${name} unbalanced`);\n}\n\nconst REGIONS = ['SetDefaults1', 'SetDefaults2', 'SetDefaults3', 'SetDefaults4', 'SetDefaults5', 'SetFoodDefaults']\n  .map((n) => methodRange(n));\n\n// ============ 表达式求值 ============\n\n/** sellPrice/buyPrice(p, g, s[, c]) = p*1e6 + g*1e4 + s*100 + c（Item.cs 同名方法同式） */\nfunction evalPrice(expr) {\n  let out = expr\n    .replace(/(?:sellPrice|buyPrice)\\(([^()]*)\\)/g, (_all, args) => {\n      const a = args.split(',').map((x) => parseInt(x.trim(), 10) || 0);\n      return String((a[0] || 0) * 1e6 + (a[1] || 0) * 1e4 + (a[2] || 0) * 100 + (a[3] || 0));\n    })\n    .replace(/\\((?:int|double|float)\\)\\s*/g, '')  // (int)((double)x * 1.5) 族强转\n    .trim();\n  if (!/^[\\d+\\-*/(). ]+$/.test(out)) return null;\n  try {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line no-new-func\n    const v = new Function(`\"use strict\";return (${out});`)();\n    return Number.isFinite(v) ? [''])[0].length;\n    if (/^(\\t*)case (\\d+):\\s*$/.test(line) && depth === 2) {\n      labels.push(parseInt(RegExp.$2, 10));\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (depth === 2 && /^\\t\\t(default:|break;|return|goto )/.test(line)) { flush(); continue; }\n    if (depth !== 3) continue;   // 只认 case 体顶层无条件语句\n    const s = line.trim();\n    let m;\n    if ((m = s.match(/^SetShopValues\\(ItemRarityColor\\.(\\w+),\\s*([^;]+)\\);$/))) {\n      const v = evalPrice(m[2]);\n      const r = RARITY_COLOR[m[1]];\n      if (r !== undefined) entries.push({ kind: 'rare', v: r });\n      if (v !== null) entries.push({ kind: 'value', v });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if ((m = s.match(/^(DefaultTo(?:SeaShell|Solution|Vanitypet|Torch|Food|HealingPotion|MusicBox))\\(/))) {\n      const f = FACTORY_VALUE[m[1]];\n      if (f) entries.push({ kind: 'value', v: f(labels[0] ?? 0) });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if ((m = s.match(/^value = ([^;]+);$/))) {\n      const v = evalPrice(m[1]);\n      if (v !== null) entries.push({ kind: 'value', v });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if ((m = s.match(/^rare = (-?\\d+);$/))) {\n      entries.push({ kind: 'rare', v: +m[1] });\n      continue;\n    }\n  }\n  flush();\n}\n\nwriteFileSync(`${ROOT}/game/src/data/vanilla-itemrare.json`, JSON.stringify(RARE));\nwriteFileSync(`${ROOT}/game/src/data/vanilla-itemvalue.json`, JSON.stringify(VALUE));\nconsole.error(`rare 条目: ${Object.keys(RARE).length} / value 条目: ${Object.keys(VALUE).length}`);\n"

If `case 1:` body is `{\n ... \n\t\t\tbreak; \n\t\t}` — the break is at 3 tabs, `}` at 2 tabs (not matched by my terminator regex `^\t\t(default:|break;|return|goto )`). Then entries keep accumulating across cases! And the last-wins would give each earlier label the LATER case's value... but that gives wrong values for the earlier ones only if flush happens at the next depth-2 terminator, assigning the accumulated entries to ALL labels including stale ones.

Look at case 1994: 
```
		case 1994:
		...
		case 2001:
		{
			DefaultToCapturedCritter(356);
			...
			break;
		}
```
Here `break;` at 3 tabs, `}` at 2 tabs. My scanner: labels [1994..2001]; entries accumulate from depth-3 statements ✓; then `break;` at depth 3 — not a terminator; `}` at depth 2 — not matched → labels NOT flushed! Then `case 2002:` pushes label 2002 (depth 2, same group — my code does `labels.push` unconditionally? No — I wrote `labels.push(parseInt(...))` — wait I didn't implement the "same depth merge" here; I just push. Since flush didn't happen, 2002 joins the same labels list. Then next depth-2 terminator flushes ALL labels [1994..2002] with combined entries — wrong!

But that produces extra entries, not the pervasive 1312 rare conflicts. The bigger issue: 5078 entries for both rare AND value — identical counts. That smells like EVERY case got both rare and value — i.e., entries accumulate globally and never flush properly (last-wins per region!). Because if flush happens rarely, each flush assigns the region's accumulated entries to the accumulated labels — but counts 5078 each... Item count ~5450? So basically most items got an entry. The "last value in region" would be the same for all labels in a giant unflushed group... but conflicts show different values per item, so partial.

Let me debug concretely: check region boundaries and what's happening with case 1.

Actually — I bet the problem is different: `methodRange` counts braces, but region [from,to] for SetDefaults1 might extend beyond the method if braces unbalanced (strings/comments). Also `LINES[i].match(/{/g)` counts braces in strings/comments. Could over-extend but not cause this.

[''])[0].length;\n    if (/^(\\t*)case (\\d+):\\s*$/.test(line) && depth === 2) {\n      labels.push(parseInt(RegExp.$2, 10));\n      continue;\n    }\n    // 体终止符（case 标签 depth 2 → 体/终止符 depth 3；内层 switch 的 break 同样\n    // 提前截断——内层 per-type 赋值属变体分支，本表建模基础变体，截断无损）\n    if (depth >= 3 && /^(break;|return\\b|goto )/.test(line.trim())) { flush(); continue; }\n    if (depth === 2 && /^\\t\\tdefault:/.test(line)) { flush(); continue; }\n    if (depth !== 3) continue;   // 只认 case 体顶层无条件语句\n    const s = line.trim();\n    let m;\n    if ((m = s.match(/^SetShopValues\\(ItemRarityColor\\.(\\w+),\\s*([^;]+)\\);$/))) {\n      const v = evalPrice(m[2]);\n      const r = RARITY_COLOR[m[1]];\n      if (r !== undefined) entries.push({ kind: 'rare', v: r });\n      if (v !== null) entries.push({ kind: 'value', v });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if ((m = s.match(/^(DefaultTo(?:SeaShell|Solution|Vanitypet|Torch|Food|HealingPotion|MusicBox))\\(/))) {\n      const f = FACTORY_VALUE[m[1]];\n      if (f) entries.push({ kind: 'value', v: f(labels[0] ?? 0) });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if ((m = s.match(/^value = ([^;]+);$/))) {\n      const v = evalPrice(m[1]);\n      if (v !== null) entries.push({ kind: 'value', v });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if ((m = s.match(/^rare = (-?\\d+);$/))) {\n      entries.push({ kind: 'rare', v: +m[1] });\n      continue;\n    }\n  }\n  flush();\n}\n",
 "new_string": "// 深度栈解析：switch 关键字 depth d → case 标签 d+1 → 体语句/终止符 d+2。\n// SetDefaults 各段嵌套异构（顶层 case depth 2；SetDefaults2/3 内还有 depth 4 的\n// 内层 switch case，如水母饵 2436-2438 :24082、贝壳 2625/2626 :25598）——\n// 按标签深度分组建栈，语句归属 depth-1 的最内层组，终止符弹同深度组；\n// 内层 per-type 赋值随内层组独立成段（变体分支，不污染外层基础值）。\nfor (const [from, to] of REGIONS) {\n  const groups = []; // { ids: number[], depth, entries: [] }\n  const groupAt = (d) => {\n    for (let i = groups.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (groups[i].depth === d) return groups[i];\n    return null;\n  };\n  const flush = (g) => {\n    if (!g) return;\n    // 顶层序列最后一个生效（DefaultToFood → SetShopValues 覆盖模式）；\n    // 工厂调用按标签逐个求值（DefaultToSeaShell 2625=500 / 2626=1000 per-type）\n    for (const id of g.ids) {\n      let val;\n      let rar;\n      for (const e of g.entries) {\n        if (e.kind === 'value') val = e.v;\n        else if (e.kind === 'rare') rar = e.v;\n        else if (e.kind === 'factory') val = FACTORY_VALUE[e.name](id);\n      }\n      if (val !== undefined) VALUE[id] = val;\n      if (rar !== undefined) RARE[id] = rar;\n    }\n    groups.splice(groups.indexOf(g), 1);\n  };\n  for (let i = from; i <= to; i++) {\n    const line = LINES[i];\n    const depth = (line.match(/^\\t*/) ?? [''])[0].length;\n    const cm = line.match(/^\\t*case (\\d+):\\s*$/);\n    if (cm) {\n      const id = parseInt(cm[1], 10);\n      const top = groups[groups.length - 1];\n      if (top && top.depth === depth) top.ids.push(id);\n      else groups.push({ ids: [id], depth, entries: [] });\n      continue;\n    }\n    const s = line.trim();\n    if (/^(break;|return\\b|goto )/.test(s)) { flush(groupAt(depth - 1)); continue; }\n    if (/^default:/.test(s)) { flush(groupAt(depth)); continue; }\n    const g = groupAt(depth - 1);\n    if (!g || depth !== g.depth + 1) continue;   // 只认 case 体顶层无条件语句\n    let m;\n    if ((m = s.match(/^SetShopValues\\(ItemRarityColor\\.(\\w+),\\s*([^;]+)\\);$/))) {\n      const v = evalPrice(m[2]);\n      const r = RARITY_COLOR[m[1]];\n      if (r !== undefined) g.entries.push({ kind: 'rare', v: r });\n      if (v !== null) g.entries.push({ kind: 'value', v });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if ((m = s.match(/^(DefaultTo(?:SeaShell|Solution|Vanitypet|Torch|Food|HealingPotion|MusicBox))\\(/))) {\n      g.entries.push({ kind: 'factory', name: m[1] });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if ((m = s.match(/^value = ([^;]+);$/))) {\n      const v = evalPrice(m[1]);\n      if (v !== null) g.entries.push({ kind: 'value', v });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if ((m = s.match(/^rare = (-?\\d+);$/))) {\n      g.entries.push({ kind: 'rare', v: +m[1] });\n    }\n  }\n  while (groups.length) flush(groups[0]);\n}\n"
}

{

 "old_string": "  spawnEnemy(key: string, x: number, y: number) {\n    // 联机访客不生成真敌（刷怪/召唤权威在房主；本地生成=幽灵实体永不同步）。\n    // 傀儡不在此列（netApplyNpcSnaps 直接入桶）\n    if (this.net && !this.net.policy.isHost) return null;\n    const e = new Enemy(key, x, y);\n    this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');\n    if (e.def.boss) this.boss = e;\n    return e;\n  }",
 "new_string": "  spawnEnemy(key: string, x: number, y: number) {\n    // 联机访客不生成真敌（刷怪/召唤权威在房主；本地生成=幽灵实体永不同步）。\n    // 傀儡不在此列（netApplyNpcSnaps 直接入桶）\n    if (this.net && !this.net.policy.isHost) return null;\n    const e = new Enemy(key, x, y);\n    this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');\n    if (e.def.boss) this.boss = e;\n    return e;\n  }\n\n  /** 按原版 NPC id 出生（小动物入微光 NPC.GetShimmered→Transform、WorldItem 放生\n   *  ReleaseNPC 的落点）。联机访客拒绝——转化权威在房主（同 spawnEnemy 门禁）。\n   *  入 critters 桶：spawnCritter 的 spawnVanilla 同语义（原版小动物表全量走桶） */\n  spawnNpcByVanilla(id: number, x: number, y: number): Enemy | null {\n    if (this.net && !this.net.policy.isHost) return null;\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, x, y);\n    if (!e) return null;\n    e.id = this.entities.nextId++;\n    this.entities.add(e, 'critters');\n    return e;\n  }"
}

 "new_string": "  /** 入微光（NPC.GetShimmered :92502-92533 小动物侧）：中心格微光液面 >30 即触发。\n   *  返回 true = 已转化（本帧 AI 终止，原版 :34596 触发后 return） */\n  private tryShimmer(game: GameHooks): boolean {\n    const st = game.world.store;\n    const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE);\n    const ty = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);\n    if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) return false;\n    const i = st.idx(tx, ty);\n    if (!(st.liquidType[i] === 4 && st.liquid[i] > 30)) return false;\n    const to = npcShimmerTransformTo(this.def.npc);\n    if (to < 0) return false;   // 无表项：分支链落空，小动物留在微光里不消散\n    this.dead = true;\n    // NPC.Transform(:81850)：贴底重定位（脚底对齐落点）；新实体微光化渐显\n    const e = game.spawnNpcByVanilla?.(to, this.cx, this.y + this.h);\n    if (e) { e.spawnAlpha = 254; e.alphaFade = true; }\n    game.playSfx('shimmerSplash', 0.75, this.cx, this.cy);\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  /** 鸭子自发行为轮盘：上岸 / 下水 / 起飞 低频切换（每 3-5 秒掷一次） */\n  private duckIdle(game: GameHooks) {"

 "new_string": "/** Item.CanShimmer（Item.cs:49045-49061）全分支 1:1：\n *  ① 月后锁 → false（:49048）\n *  ② 560 史莱姆王冠且雨进行中 → false（:49052）\n *  ③ (:49056) 无转化目标 ∧ 不可分解 ∧ 非钱币 ∧ (非 4986 或已解锁彩虹史莱姆)\n *     → 只剩 makeNPC > 0 一条路（:49058）——4986 未解锁时特例放行（:49060）\n *  decraftCtx 缺省 = 不启用 decraft 判定（调用方旧签名兼容） */\nexport function canShimmerItem(\n  vid: number, downedMoonlord = false, moonPhase?: number,\n  decraftCtx?: { stack: number; crimsonWorld: boolean; downedBoss3: boolean; downedGolemBoss: boolean },\n  shimmerCtx?: { slimeRain?: boolean; rainbowSlimeUnlocked?: boolean },\n): boolean {\n  if (isItemTransformLocked(vid, downedMoonlord)) return false;\n  if (vid === 560 && shimmerCtx?.slimeRain) return false;\n  const special4986 = vid === 4986 && !shimmerCtx?.rainbowSlimeUnlocked; // :49056 反例\n  if (!special4986\n    && getTransformToItem(vid, moonPhase) <= 0\n    && !COMMON_COIN.has(vid)\n    && !(decraftCtx && isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked(vid, decraftCtx.stack, decraftCtx.crimsonWorld, decraftCtx.downedBoss3, decraftCtx.downedGolemBoss))) {\n    return makeNpcOfItem(vid) > 0;\n  }\n  return true;\n}\n\n/** Item.makeNPC（Item.cs:47613-47626 DefaultToCapturedCritter）：瓶中小动物物品 →\n *  放生 NPC id。tools/extract-shimmer.mjs 从 DefaultToCapturedCritter 调用点提取\n *  （含 (short)(type - 4334 + 595) 族工厂式逐标签求值），0 = 非放生物品 */\nexport function makeNpcOfItem(vid: number): number {\n  return ITEM_MAKE_NPC[vid] ?? 0;\n}"
}

describe('选取规则：IsCrafted last-wins + 邪恶覆盖分支', () => {

    const corr = decraftOutcome(556, 1, false)!;
    expect(corr.piles).toEqual([{ vid: 68, stack: 6 }, { vid: 22, stack: 5 }, { vid: 521, stack: 6 }]);
    const crim = decraftOutcome(556, 1, true)!;
    expect(crim.piles).toEqual([{ vid: 1330, stack: 6 }, { vid: 22, stack: 5 }, { vid: 521, stack: 6 }]);
  });

  it('怪物千层面 5092：8 腐烂块 ↔ 8 椎骨', () => {
    expect(decraftOutcome(5092, 1, false)!.piles).toEqual([{ vid: 68, stack: 8 }]);
    expect(decraftOutcome(5092, 1, true)!.piles).toEqual([{ vid: 1330, stack: 8 }]);

interface Spawned { key: string; count: number; drop: ItemDrop }

function hooks(w: World, p: Player, spawned: Spawned[]): GameHooks {
  return {
    world: w, player: p,
    enemies: () => [], critters: () => [],
    spawnDrop: (x: number, y: number, key: string, count?: number) => {
      const d = new ItemDrop(x, y, ITEM_BY_KEY[key], count ?? 1);
      spawned.push({ key, count: count ?? 1, drop: d });
      return d;
    },
    damagePlayer: () => {}, addDamageNumber: () => {}, cutTile: () => {},
    onEnemyKilled: () => {}, spawnEnemy: () => {},
    spawnParticles: () => {}, notifyInventoryChanged: () => {},
    playSfx: () => {}, playSfxFiles: () => {}, showPickupLabel: () => {},
  } as unknown as GameHooks;
}

// ============ 端到端（ItemDrop 入微光池 → GetShimmered 分支顺序） ============

const W = 60, H = 60;

function shimmerWorld(): World {
  const w = new World(W, H, 11, 'decraft-test');
  const st = w.store;
  for (let x = 10; x < 30; x++) {
    st.setTile(x, 40, 1);
    for (let y = 33; y < 40; y++) st.setLiquid(x, y, 255, 4);
  }
  w.lavaLine = 50;
  return w;
}

		{
			List<IBestiaryInfoElement> commonFilters = BestiaryDatabaseNPCsPopulator.CommonTags.GetCommonInfoElementsForFilters();
			List<KeyValuePair<int, NPC>> list = (from x in NpcsByNetId.ToList()
				orderby GetBestiaryTownPriority(x.Value), !x.Value.isLikeATownNPC, GetBestiaryNormalGoldCritterPriority(x.Value), !x.Value.CountsAsACritter, GetBestiaryBossPriority(x.Value), GetLowestBiomeGroupIndex(x.Value, database, commonFilters), x.Value.aiStyle, GetBestiaryPowerLevel(x.Value), GetBestiaryStarsPriority(x.Value)
				select x).ToList();
			list.RemoveAll((KeyValuePair<int, NPC> x) => ShouldHideBestiaryEntry(x.Value));
			return list;
		}

		public static int GetLowestBiomeGroupIndex(NPC npc, BestiaryDatabase database, List<IBestiaryInfoElement> commonElements)
		{
			List<IBestiaryInfoElement> info = database.FindEntryByNPCID(npc.netID).Info;
			for (int num = commonElements.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				if (info.IndexOf(commonElements[num]) != -1)
				{
					return num;
				}
			}
			return int.MaxValue;
		}

		public static bool ShouldHideBestiaryEntry(NPC npc)
		{
			if (NPCID.Sets.NPCBestiaryDrawOffset.TryGetValue(npc.netID, out var value))
			{
				return value.Hide;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static float GetBestiaryPowerLevel(NPC npc)
		{
			return npc.damage + npc.defense + npc.lifeMax / 4;
		}

		public static int GetBestiaryTownPriority(NPC npc)
		{
			int num = NPCID.Sets.TownNPCBestiaryPriority.IndexOf(npc.netID);
			if (num == -1)
			{
				num = int.MaxValue;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public static int GetBestiaryNormalGoldCritterPriority(NPC npc)
		{
			int num = NPCID.Sets.NormalGoldCritterBestiaryPriority.IndexOf(npc.netID);
			if (num == -1)
			{
				num = int.MaxValue;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public static int GetBestiaryBossPriority(NPC npc)
		{
			return NPCID.Sets.BossBestiaryPriority.IndexOf(npc.netID);
		}

```

	private static int GetNPCBestiaryRarityStarsCount(NPC npc)
	{
		float num = 1f;
		num += (float)npc.rarity;
		if (npc.rarity == 1)
		{
			num += 1f;
		}
		else if (npc.rarity == 2)
		{
			num += 1.5f;
		}
		else if (npc.rarity == 3)
		{
			num += 2f;
		}
		else if (npc.rarity == 4)
		{
			num += 2.5f;
		}
		else if (npc.rarity == 5)
		{
			num += 3f;
		}
		else if (npc.rarity > 0)
		{
			num += 3.5f;
		}
		if (npc.boss)
		{
			num += 0.5f;
		}
		int num2 = npc.damage + npc.defense + npc.lifeMax / 4;
		if (num2 > 10000)
		{
			num += 3.5f;
		}
		else if (num2 > 5000)
		{
			num += 3f;
		}
		else if (num2 > 1000)
		{
			num += 2.5f;
		}
		else if (num2 > 500)
		{
			num += 2f;
		}
		else if (num2 > 150)
		{
			num += 1.5f;
		}
		else if (num2 > 50)
		{
			num += 1f;
		}
		if (num > 5f)
		{
			num = 5f;
		}
		return (int)num;
	}

	private static void CreateCreativeItemSortingIds()
	{
		CreativeHelper.SetCreativeMenuOrder();
	}

```
/** 分页数（探针口径：546 条 / 每页条数 → 页数） */
export function bestiaryPageCount(total: number, perPage: number): number {
  if (perPage <= 0) return 0;
  return Math.ceil(total / perPage);
}

export type BestiarySortKey = 'unlocks' | 'id' | 'alpha' | 'attack' | 'defense' | 'coins' | 'hp';
/** SortingSteps 注册序（RegisterSortSteps）——本面板可选步（缺 BestiaryID/Rarity 两步） */
export const BESTIARY_SORT_KEYS: BestiarySortKey[] = ['unlocks', 'id', 'alpha', 'attack', 'defense', 'coins', 'hp'];

export function sortLabel(key: BestiarySortKey): string {
  switch (key) {
    case 'unlocks': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_Unlocks');
    case 'id': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_ID');
    case 'alpha': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_Alphabetical');
    case 'attack': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_Attack');
    case 'defense': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_Defense');
    case 'coins': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_Coins');
    case 'hp': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_HitPoints');
  }
}

export interface BestiaryFilter {
  search: string;
  /** 条目类别（本仓自有维度，无原版键） */
  kind: 'all' | 'enemy' | 'town' | 'critter';
  /** Filters.ByBoss（BestiaryInfo.IsBoss） */
  boss: boolean;
  /** Filters.ByUnlockState（BestiaryInfo.IfUnlocked）；'no' 为反向（本仓补充） */
  unlocked: 'all' | 'yes' | 'no';
}

export const DEFAULT_BESTIARY_FILTER: BestiaryFilter = { search: '', kind: 'all', boss: false, unlocked: 'all' };

export interface BestiaryRow {
  entry: BestiaryEntryDef;
  /** 条目代表 NPC id（图标/属性取自它；归并族取母体） */
  npcId: number;
  state: UnlockState;
  name: string;
  /** 击杀数（kill 来源 creditId 的计数；非击杀条目 0） */
  kills: number;
}

/** creditId → 母体 NPC id（BESTIARY_CREDIT_REDIRECT 归并族取未被重定向者，正 id 优先） */
let repIdCache: Map<string, number> | null = null;
export function bestiaryRepNpcId(creditId: string): number {
  if (!repIdCache) {
    repIdCache = new Map();
    const R = BESTIARY_CREDIT_REDIRECT as Record<number, number>;
    const put = (id: number) => {
      const cid = NPC_NAME_BY_ID[id] ?? String(id);
      if (!repIdCache!.has(cid)) repIdCache!.set(cid, id);
    };
    for (const key of Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)) {
      const id = Number(key);
      if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id === 0) continue;
      if (R[id] === undefined && id > 0) put(id);   // 母体（正 id）
    }
    for (const key of Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)) {
      const id = Number(key);
      if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id === 0) continue;
      if (R[id] !== undefined) put(R[id]);           // 变体族回填母体
    }
    for (const key of Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)) {
      const id = Number(key);
      // 负 netID 变体（史莱姆配色 -1..-10 / 世吞段 -11..-13）代表自身
      if (Number.isInteger(id) && id !== 0) put(id);
    }
  }
  const fallback = Number(creditId);
  return repIdCache.get(creditId) ?? (Number.isFinite(fallback) ? fallback : 0);
}

/** BestiaryEntry.Enemy（Populator）+ NPCID.Sets.ShouldBeCountedAsBossForBestiary
 *  的 creditId 集（Boss 筛选用；Enemy.ts VANILLA_BOSS_IDS 同表） */
const BOSS_CREDITS = new Set([
  4, 13, 35, 50, 68, 113, 125, 126, 127, 134, 222, 245, 262, 266, 370, 395, 398,
  439, 636, 657, 325, 327, 345, 346, 517, 422, 507, 493, 664,
].map(bestiaryCreditId));

export function isBossCredit(creditId: string): boolean {
  return BOSS_CREDITS.has(creditId);
}

/** 全条目行化（bestiaryEntries × tracker.unlockState × 名字解析） */
export function bestiaryRows(tracker: BestiaryTracker): BestiaryRow[] {
  const out: BestiaryRow[] = [];
  for (const entry of bestiaryEntries()) {
    let kills = 0;
    for (const s of entry.sources) {
      if (s.t === 'kill') kills = Math.max(kills, tracker.kills.get(s.credit) ?? 0);
    }
    const npcId = bestiaryRepNpcId(entry.creditId);
    const state = tracker.unlockState(entry);
    out.push({
      entry, npcId, kills, state,
      // 未解锁不显示名（原版图标 hover "???"）
      name: state > UnlockState.NotKnown ? npcDisplayName(npcId, entry.creditId) : '???',
    });
  }
  return out;
}

/** 条目显示名：正 id 走 Lang.npcName；负 netID（史莱姆/世吞段变体）Lang.npcName
 *  会取 abs 误命中他者，改按内部名直查；都缺回退 creditId */
export function npcDisplayName(npcId: number, fallback: string): string {
  if (npcId > 0) return Lang.npcName(npcId) ?? fallback;
  const internal = NPC_NAME_BY_ID[npcId];
  if (internal && Lang.has(`NPCName.${internal}`)) return Lang.text(`NPCName.${internal}`);
  return fallback;
}

/** 排序（SortingSteps 各 Compare 的数据层等价；无数据步缺省，平手按 npcId 升序） */
export function sortBestiaryRows(rows: BestiaryRow[], key: BestiarySortKey): void {
  const stat = (r: BestiaryRow, f: 'lifeMax' | 'damage' | 'defense'): number =>
    VANILLA_NPCS[String(r.npcId)]?.[f] ?? -1;
  const cmp: Record<BestiarySortKey, (a: BestiaryRow, b: BestiaryRow) => number> = {
    // ByUnlockState：解锁档降序（已解锁在前）
    unlocks: (a, b) => b.state - a.state || a.npcId - b.npcId,
    // ByNetId 升序
    id: (a, b) => a.npcId - b.npcId,
    // Alphabetical：显示名升序
    alpha: (a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'zh') || a.npcId - b.npcId,
    // ByStat 族：降序（无数据条目沉底）
    attack: (a, b) => stat(b, 'damage') - stat(a, 'damage') || a.npcId - b.npcId,
    defense: (a, b) => stat(b, 'defense') - stat(a, 'defense') || a.npcId - b.npcId,
    coins: (a, b) => npcValueOf(b.npcId) - npcValueOf(a.npcId) || a.npcId - b.npcId,
    hp: (a, b) => stat(b, 'lifeMax') - stat(a, 'lifeMax') || a.npcId - b.npcId,
  };
  rows.sort(cmp[key]);
}

/** 筛选（Filters.BySearch/ByUnlockState/ByBoss + 本仓 kind 维度） */
export function filterBestiaryRows(rows: BestiaryRow[], f: BestiaryFilter): BestiaryRow[] {
  const q = f.search.trim().toLowerCase();
  return rows.filter((r) => {
    if (f.kind !== 'all' && r.entry.kind !== f.kind) return false;
    if (f.boss && !isBossCredit(r.entry.creditId)) return false;
    if (f.unlocked === 'yes' && r.state === UnlockState.NotKnown) return false;
    if (f.unlocked === 'no' && r.state > UnlockState.NotKnown) return false;
    // BySearch：未解锁条目不提供搜索串（原版 NamePlate 隐藏）
    if (q && (r.state === UnlockState.NotKnown || !r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q))) return false;
    return true;
  });
}

// ============ 掉落表展平（ItemDropBestiaryInfoElement 近似） ============

export interface DropLine {

type Source =

export interface BestiaryEntryDef {
  creditId: string;
  kind: 'enemy' | 'town' | 'critter';
  sources: Source[];
  /** 金小动物门：任一来源解锁后仍需全图已见至少一只金种（GoldCritterUICollectionInfoProvider） */
  goldGate: boolean;
}

function killSource(npcId: number, quick: boolean): Source {
  return {
    t: 'kill',
    credit: bestiaryCreditId(npcId),
    quick,
    full: KILLS_TO_FULL[npcId] ?? BESTIARY_DEFAULT_FULL_KILLS,
  };
}
const sightSource = (npcId: number): Source => ({ t: 'sight', credit: bestiaryCreditId(npcId) });
const chatSource = (npcId: number): Source => ({ t: 'chat', credit: bestiaryCreditId(npcId) });

/** 全量 NPC 的 boss 旗（Enemy.ts VANILLA_BOSS_IDS 同表；BestiaryEntry.Enemy :37 quickUnlock 判定） */
const VANILLA_BOSS_IDS = new Set([
  4, 13, 35, 50, 68, 113, 125, 126, 127, 134, 222, 245, 262, 266, 370, 395, 398, 439, 636, 657,
  325, 327, 345, 346,
]);

let entryCache: BestiaryEntryDef[] | null = null;

/** 条目注册表（BestiaryDatabaseNPCsPopulator.Populate 的数据层等价；惰性构建一次） */
export function bestiaryEntries(): BestiaryEntryDef[] {
  if (entryCache) return entryCache;
  const out: BestiaryEntryDef[] = [];
  for (const key of Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)) {
    const id = Number(key);
    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id === 0 || HIDE.has(id)) continue;
    const isTown = BESTIARY_TOWN_IDS.has(id);
    // AddEmptyEntries_CrittersAndEnemies_Automated：isLikeATownNPC 走手工城镇表，

    const kind: BestiaryEntryDef['kind'] = isTown ? 'town' : countsAsACritter(id) ? 'critter' : 'enemy';
    let sources: Source[];
    let goldGate = false;
    const goldNormals = GOLD_CRITTER_NORMALS[id];
    if (goldNormals) {
      // 金小动物：本体遇 + 同族普通种遇；解锁态恒 0/4
      sources = [sightSource(id), ...goldNormals.map(sightSource)];
      goldGate = true;
    } else if (id in CRITTER_PAIR) {
      sources = [sightSource(id), sightSource(CRITTER_PAIR[id])];
    } else if (id in MULTI_SOURCES) {
      sources = MULTI_SOURCES[id].map((s) =>
        s === 'kill' ? killSource(id, isQuick(id))
          : s === 'chat' ? chatSource(id)
            : s[1] === 'kill' ? killSource(s[0], isQuick(s[0])) : chatSource(s[0]));
    } else if (id in KILL_CREDIT_FROM) {
      // 击杀记账归并族（Populator :600-616）：读母体杀数，quickUnlock=true
      sources = [killSource(KILL_CREDIT_FROM[id], true)];
    } else if (kind === 'town') {
      sources = [chatSource(id)];
    } else if (kind === 'critter') {
      sources = [sightSource(id)];
    } else {
      sources = [killSource(id, isQuick(id))];
    }
    out.push({ creditId: bestiaryCreditId(id), kind, sources, goldGate });
  }
  entryCache = out;
  return out;
}

function isQuick(id: number): boolean {
  return VANILLA_BOSS_IDS.has(id) || COUNTED_AS_BOSS.has(id) || QUICK_UNLOCK_IDS.has(id);
}

 "content": "// 图鉴排序/稀有度静态表提取（1.4.5.6 = Terarria1456）→ src/data/vanilla-bestiary.json。\n//   - NPCID.cs Sets 三张优先级表（GetSortedBestiaryEntriesList orderby 链的键，ContentSamples.cs:745-753）：\n//       TownNPCBestiaryPriority            :4427\n//       NormalGoldCritterBestiaryPriority  :4407\n//       BossBestiaryPriority               :4416\n//   - NPC.cs SetDefaults if/else-if 链的 rarity 赋值（GetNPCBestiaryRarityStarsCount\n//     公式入参，ContentSamples.cs:1223-1285；链式条件 `else if (type == X || ...)` 逐 id 登记）\n//   - ContentSamples.cs FillNpcRarities 显式覆盖表（:1084-1220，循环公式算完后再覆写，\n//     城镇 NPC 钳 1-3 / Boss 钳 4-5）→ rarityStarOverrides\n// 注意：src/data/bestiaryStatics.generated.ts 头注亦署名本脚本，但其原始生成逻辑已遗失\n//   （本仓仅存产物）；本脚本**不**重写该文件，只产出本 JSON。\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';\n\nconst ROOT = '~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld';\nconst read = (p) => readFileSync(`${ROOT}/${p}`, 'utf8');\n\nconst npcid = read('Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/NPCID.cs');\nconst npccs = read('Terarria1456/Terraria/NPC.cs');\nconst samples = read('Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/ContentSamples.cs');\n\n/** `public static List<int> NAME = new List<int> { ... };` 数字表 */\nfunction intList(src, name) {\n  const m = src.match(new RegExp(`public static List<int> ${name} = new List<int>\\\\s*\\\\{([\\\\s\\\\S]*?)\\\\};`));\n  if (!m) throw new Error(`list ${name} not found`);\n  return m[1].split(/[,\\s]+/).map((s) => parseInt(s, 10)).filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n));\n}\n\n/** NPC.cs SetDefaults if/else-if 链的 rarity：`else if (type == 45)` / `type == 46 || type == 303`\n *  块内首条 `rarity = N;`（块内无条件单赋值，首条即终值） */\nfunction npcRarityMap(src) {\n  const out = {};\n  const lines = src.split('\\n');\n  let ids = null;            // 当前条件块命中的 id 列表（null = 链外）\n  for (const line of lines) {\n    const depth = (line.match(/^\\t*/) ?? [''])[0].length;\n    const s = line.trim();\n    const cond = s.match(/^(?:else )?if \\(type (.+?)\\)\\s*\\{?$/);\n    if (cond && depth === 2) {\n      ids = [...cond[1].matchAll(/==\\s*(-?\\d+)/g)].map((m) => parseInt(m[1], 10));\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (ids && depth === 3) {\n      const r = s.match(/^rarity = (\\d+);$/);\n      if (r) {\n        for (const id of ids) out[id] = parseInt(r[1], 10);\n        ids = null;\n        continue;\n      }\n    }\n    if (depth <= 1) ids = null;   // 块结束离开 SetDefaults 链\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** ContentSamples.FillNpcRarities 显式覆盖（`NpcBestiaryRarityStars[id] = N;`） */\nfunction rarityStarOverrides(src) {\n  const m = src.match(/private static void FillNpcRarities\\(\\)([\\s\\S]*?)private static int GetNPCBestiaryRarityStarsCount/);\n  if (!m) throw new Error('FillNpcRarities not found');\n  const out = {};\n  for (const hit of m[1].matchAll(/NpcBestiaryRarityStars\\[(-?\\d+)\\] = (\\d+);/g)) {\n    out[hit[1]] = parseInt(hit[2], 10);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nconst data = {\n  townNpcBestiaryPriority: intList(npcid, 'TownNPCBestiaryPriority'),\n  normalGoldCritterBestiaryPriority: intList(npcid, 'NormalGoldCritterBestiaryPriority'),\n  bossBestiaryPriority: intList(npcid, 'BossBestiaryPriority'),\n  npcRarity: npcRarityMap(npccs),\n  rarityStarOverrides: rarityStarOverrides(samples),\n};\n\nwriteFileSync(`${ROOT}/game/src/data/vanilla-bestiary.json`, JSON.stringify(data));\nfor (const [k, v] of Object.entries(data)) {\n  const n = Array.isArray(v) ? v.length : Object.keys(v).length;\n  console.error(`${k}: ${n} 条`);\n}\n"
}
```

export function bestiaryRarityStars(netId: number): number {

  const v = VANILLA_NPCS[String(netId)];

  let n = 1 + rarity;
  if (rarity === 1) n += 1; else if (rarity === 2) n += 1.5; else if (rarity === 3) n += 2; else if (rarity === 4) n += 2.5; else if (rarity === 5) n += 3; else if (rarity > 0) n += 3.5;
  if (boss) n += 0.5;
  const power = (v?.damage ?? 0) + (v?.defense ?? 0) + Math.floor((v?.lifeMax ?? 0) / 4);

}

```ts
let sortingIdCache: Map<number, number> | null = null;
export function bestiarySortingId(netId: number): number {
  if (!sortingIdCache) {
    sortingIdCache = new Map();
    const ids = Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID).map(Number).filter(n => Number.isInteger(n));
    const key = (id: number): number[] => [townPriority, likeTown?0:1, goldPriority, counts?0:1, bossPriority, 0/*biome—registered*/, aiStyle, power(fixed-point), stars];
    ids.sort((a,b) => compare keys then id);
    ids.forEach((id, i) => sortingIdCache.set(id, i+1));
  }
  return sortingIdCache.get(netId) ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
}
```

 "command": "cat >> src/data/Bestiary.ts <<'EOF'\n\n// ============ 排序 id + 稀有度星（ContentSamples.NpcBestiarySortingId / NpcBestiaryRarityStars） ============\n// 数据来源 tools/extract-bestiary.mjs → vanilla-bestiary.json：\n//   TownNPCBestiaryPriority :4427 / NormalGoldCritterBestiaryPriority :4407 /\n//   BossBestiaryPriority :4416（GetSortedBestiaryEntriesList orderby 三键）+\n//   NPC.cs SetDefaults if 链 rarity 赋值 + ContentSamples.FillNpcRarities 显式覆盖(:1084-1220)\nimport bestiaryJson from './vanilla-bestiary.json';\n\ntype BestiaryJson = typeof bestiaryJson;\nconst TOWN_PRIORITY: number[] = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).townNpcBestiaryPriority;\nconst GOLD_CRITTER_PRIORITY: number[] = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).normalGoldCritterBestiaryPriority;\nconst BOSS_PRIORITY: number[] = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).bossBestiaryPriority;\nconst NPC_RARITY: Record<string, number> = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).npcRarity;\nconst STAR_OVERRIDES: Record<string, number> = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).rarityStarOverrides;\n\n/** NPC.isLikeATownNPC（NPC.cs:6772-6781）：453 恒真，其余走 townNPC 旗 */\nexport function isLikeATownNpc(npcId: number): boolean {\n  return npcId === 453 || BESTIARY_TOWN_IDS.has(npcId);\n}\n\n/** ContentSamples.GetNPCBestiaryRarityStarsCount（:1223-1285）1:1：\n *  1 + rarity（1→+1 / 2→+1.5 / 3→+2 / 4→+2.5 / 5→+3 / >5→+3.5）+ boss 0.5 +\n *  战力阈（>50→+1 / >150→+1.5 / >500→+2 / >1000→+2.5 / >5000→+3 / >10000→+3.5），\n *  钳 5 取整。FillNpcRarities 先全量算公式再显式覆盖（城镇 1-3 / Boss 4-5 段），\n *  覆盖优先。stats 缺表（1405 json 缺 id）按 0 计——与原版 SetDefaults 后的\n *  实际值可能偏差，登记待办 */\nexport function bestiaryRarityStars(npcId: number): number {\n  const o = STAR_OVERRIDES[npcId];\n  if (o !== undefined) return o;\n  const v = VANILLA_NPCS[String(npcId)];\n  const rarity = NPC_RARITY[npcId] ?? 0;\n  const boss = VANILLA_BOSS_IDS.has(npcId);\n  let n = 1 + rarity;\n  if (rarity === 1) n += 1;\n  else if (rarity === 2) n += 1.5;\n  else if (rarity === 3) n += 2;\n  else if (rarity === 4) n += 2.5;\n  else if (rarity === 5) n += 3;\n  else if (rarity > 0) n += 3.5;\n  if (boss) n += 0.5;\n  const power = (v?.damage ?? 0) + (v?.defense ?? 0) + Math.floor((v?.lifeMax ?? 0) / 4);\n  if (power > 10000) n += 3.5;\n  else if (power > 5000) n += 3;\n  else if (power > 1000) n += 2.5;\n  else if (power > 500) n += 2;\n  else if (power > 150) n += 1.5;\n  else if (power > 50) n += 1;\n  return Math.trunc(Math.min(n, 5));\n}\n\nlet sortingIdCache: Map<number, number> | null = null;\n\n/** ContentSamples.CreateBestiarySortingIds（:1063-1073）——GetSortedBestiaryEntriesList\n *  （BestiaryHelper，ContentSamples.cs:745-753）九键 orderby 链的稳定全序 → 1..N 序号：\n *  城镇优先位 → !isLikeATownNPC → 金小动物优先位 → !CountsAsACritter → Boss 优先位 →\n *  群系组序 → aiStyle → 战力 → 星数（全升序；IndexOf 缺席 = MAX，Boss 缺席 = -1）。\n *  ⚠️ 第 6 键 GetLowestBiomeGroupIndex（:762-772）未提取——需 BestiaryDatabase 逐 NPC\n *  的 SpawnCondition 过滤元素表（BestiaryDatabaseNPCsPopulator 逐条注册，无静态源），\n *  此处以常量 0 代入：同档普通敌怪的组内相对序与原版有偏差，登记待办 */\nexport function bestiarySortingId(npcId: number): number {\n  if (!sortingIdCache) {\n    sortingIdCache = new Map();\n    const townIdx = new Map(TOWN_PRIORITY.map((id, i) => [id, i]));\n    const goldIdx = new Map(GOLD_CRITTER_PRIORITY.map((id, i) => [id, i]));\n    const bossIdx = new Map(BOSS_PRIORITY.map((id, i) => [id, i]));\n    const MAX = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;\n    const sortKey = (id: number): number[] => {\n      const v = VANILLA_NPCS[String(id)];\n      return [\n        townIdx.get(id) ?? MAX,                 // GetBestiaryTownPriority\n        isLikeATownNpc(id) ? 0 : 1,             // !isLikeATownNPC\n        goldIdx.get(id) ?? MAX,                 // GetBestiaryNormalGoldCritterPriority\n        countsAsACritter(id) ? 0 : 1,           // !CountsAsACritter\n        bossIdx.get(id) ?? -1,                  // GetBestiaryBossPriority\n        0,                                      // GetLowestBiomeGroupIndex（未提取，登记）\n        v?.aiStyle ?? 0,\n        ((v?.damage ?? 0) + (v?.defense ?? 0) + Math.floor((v?.lifeMax ?? 0) / 4)) * 4, // 战力 ×4 保整\n        bestiaryRarityStars(id),\n      ];\n    };\n    const ids = Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)\n      .map(Number)\n      .filter((n) => Number.isInteger(n) && n !== 0);\n    ids.sort((a, b) => {\n      const ka = sortKey(a);\n      const kb = sortKey(b);\n      for (let i = 0; i < ka.length; i++) {\n        if (ka[i] !== kb[i]) return ka[i] - kb[i];\n      }\n      return a - b;   // 全键平手：稳定序（原版 List 稳定排序保持注册序）\n    });\n    ids.forEach((id, i) => sortingIdCache!.set(id, i + 1));\n  }\n  return sortingIdCache.get(npcId) ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;\n}\nEOF\nnpx tsc --noEmit 2>&1 | grep -E \"Bestiary\" | head",
 "description": "Add sorting id + rarity stars to Bestiary.ts"
}

// 多人联机共享协议层（客户端与 Node 服务器共用，docs/multiplayer-design.md §1-2）。
// 帧格式对齐原版：[u16 len][u8 msgId][payload...]，小端，包上限 65535B。
// 消息 ID 尽量沿用原版 MessageID 编号（Hello=1/SLOT=3/WorldData=7/TileSection=10/
// PlayerSpawn=12/PlayerControls=13/TileManipulation=17/SetTime=18/NetModules=82/Ping=154）；
// 编码字段为本仓库简化集（protoVer 门禁，当前 v6——演进史见 PROTO_VER 注释）。

export const PROTO_MAGIC = 'SW1';
/** v2：msg13 对齐原版 PlayerControls 布局（控制位+position+velocity）。
 *  v3：实体同步族（msg16/21/23/27/42）+ 箱子权威（msg31/32）+ sessionToken 重连
 *  v4：msg23 S→C 短码格式（key 每次全量→codeId 短码+按需全量）+ RoomPolicy 尾部 u16 maxPlayers
 *  v5：msg27 kind 高位 bit7=hostile（原版 Projectile.hostile 位——访客端据此对敌对弹幕
 *      傀儡本地结算 Damage_EVP；服务器剥除非房主置位）
 *  v6：msg27 kind 5-8（锚定 Boss 的自包含弹体：DukeSharknadoBolt/龙卷/MLDeathray/LunarOrb，
 *      tag 打包锚参数，访客重建真类本地跑确定性 AI）+ msg28 弹幕消亡上报（消幽灵弹窗口）+
 *      msg201 SSC 角色档下发（--ssc）。server 与 game 同仓库同时发布，不做向后兼容 */
export const PROTO_VER = 6;

/** 消息 ID（v1 实现范围；编号对齐原版 MessageID.cs） */
export const enum Msg {
  Hello = 1,          // C→S {magic, protoVer, name, hostToken 兜底}
  Kick = 2,           // S→C {reason}（原版 Kick/Disconnect 同号）
  PlayerSlot = 3,     // S→C {slot, sessionToken}（原版同号 PlayerInfo=3：名字/外观在原版
                      //   走此包，我们拆到 Hello+msg4——字段分布简化，ID 保留；
                      //   sessionToken 为重连凭据）
  SyncPlayer = 4,     // 双向 {slot, appearanceJson}
  SyncPlayerItem = 5, // C→S→广播（v2 对齐原版 msg5 SyncPlayerItem 语义，批量变体）：
                      //   {u8 count, entries[{u8 playerSlot(服务端覆写), u8 container,
                      //    u8 itemSlot, u16 itemId(0=空), u16 stack}]}
                      //   container: 0=slots[0..57] 1=armor[0..19] 2=dye[0..9]
  RequestWorldData = 6, // C→S {}
  WorldData = 7,      // S→C {时间/尺寸/出生点/层线/flags/seed/name}
  SpawnTileData = 8,  // C→S {x, y}（客户端请求出生点周围 section）
  StatusText = 9,     // S→C {count}（将发的 strip 数，进度条）
  TileSection = 10,   // S→C {x0,y0,w,h, rleBytes}（200×20 条带）
  PlayerSpawn = 12,   // S→C {slot, x, y}（进房落点确认）
  PlayerState = 13,   // C→S→广播（v2 对齐原版 msg13）：{u8 slot, u8 ctrlBits, u8 flagBits,
                      //   u8 selectedItem, f32 x, f32 y, [f32 vx, f32 vy 若 flagBits[2]]}
                      //   ctrlBits: [0]up [1]down [2]left [3]right [4]jump [5]useItem [6]direction
                      //   flagBits: [2]hasVelocity [6]ghost(死亡)。position=碰撞盒左上（原版语义）
  PlayerActive = 14,  // S→C 广播 {slot, active, name}

// 自然层粒子 1:1：落叶（Gore SpecialAI 3 / Gore_UpdateLeaf :1104-1215）+ 滴水
//（Gore IsDrip 状态机 :383-530 + 碰撞转溅落 :955-985 + EmitLiquidDrops TileDrawing :7714）。
// 贴图 = Gore_N.png 懒加载（vanilla-atlas MISC 段已拷入 public/sprites/vanilla/）。
// 物理逐分支对源：落叶下落用 vy 兼作相位累加器走圆弧（UnitY.RotatedBy(vy)=(−sin,cos)，
// Y 取 |·|，地表加风力 ×4），碰撞 → vy=-1 转落地滑移/褪色支；滴水 0-6 蓄滴
//（随机等待+上方活性检查）→ 5 帧生成下落子滴(frame9) → 7-9 加速下落 → 碰撞/入水转 10-14 溅落。
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import type { SpriteAtlas } from '../assets/SpriteAtlas';
import type { TileStore } from '../world/TileStore';

// GoreID.Sets.IsDrip（GoreID.cs :15）
export const DRIP_GORES = new Set([706, 714, 713, 707, 715, 711, 1147, 709, 710, 708, 712, 716, 717, 943, 1160, 1161, 1162, 1383]);
// GoreID.Sets.SpecialAI==3（:9 落叶族）——SpriteFrame(32,8)：32 列=油漆色 × 8 行=旋转帧
export const LEAF_GORES = new Set([
  ...Array.from({ length: 925 - 910 + 1 }, (_, i) => 910 + i),
  ...Array.from({ length: 1121 - 1113 + 1 }, (_, i) => 1113 + i),
  ...Array.from({ length: 1255 - 1248 + 1 }, (_, i) => 1248 + i),
  1257, 1278,
]);

interface Leaf {
  x: number; y: number;
  vx: number; vy: number;     // vy<0 = 落地支（Gore_UpdateLeaf 分支条件）
  row: number; rowGroup: number; rowCounter: number;
  alpha: number; timeLeft: number;
  scale: number; gore: number;
  dead: boolean;
}

interface Drip {
  x: number; y: number;
  vy: number;
  type: number;
  frame: number; frameCounter: number;
  alpha: number;
  dead: boolean;
}

/** 盒碰撞（Gore 走 Collision.TileCollision 3-14px 盒）：逐轴探测，撞轴归零并回报 */
function boxCollide(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, size: number): { nx: number; ny: number; nvx: number; nvy: number; hitX: boolean; hitY: boolean } {
  let nx = x, ny = y, hitX = false, hitY = false;
  if (vx !== 0) {
    const edge = vx > 0 ? x + size + vx : x + vx;
    const tx = Math.floor(edge / TILE);
    let blocked = false;
    for (let ty = Math.floor((y + 1) / TILE); ty <= Math.floor((y + size - 1) / TILE); ty++) {
      if (st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { blocked = true; break; }
    }
    if (blocked) { hitX = true; } else nx = x + vx;
  }
  if (vy !== 0) {
    const edge = vy > 0 ? y + size + vy : y + vy;
    const ty = Math.floor(edge / TILE);
    let blocked = false;
    for (let tx = Math.floor((x + 1) / TILE); tx <= Math.floor((x + size - 1) / TILE); tx++) {
      if (st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { blocked = true; break; }
    }
    if (blocked) { hitY = true; } else ny = y + vy;
  }
  return { nx, ny, nvx: hitX ? 0 : vx, nvy: hitY ? 0 : vy, hitX, hitY };
}

export class NatureParticles {
  leaves: Leaf[] = [];
  drips: Drip[] = [];
  private st: TileStore | null = null;
  maxLeaves = 220;
  maxDrips = 90;
  /** 滴水溅落音钩子（Game 注入 Sfx；Gore.cs :971-984：SoundID 39=Drip，
   *  落地 variant rand(0,1) / 入水 variant 2，位置 +8,+8；flag4 水型本模块内静音） */
  onDripSplash: ((x: number, y: number, wet: boolean) => void) | null = null;

  clear(): void { this.leaves.length = 0; this.drips.length = 0; }
  /** Game 每帧绑定当前世界 store */
  bindStore(st: TileStore): void { this.st = st; }

  private liquidAt(px: number, py: number): number {
    const st = this.st;
    if (!st) return 0;
    const tx = Math.floor(px / TILE), ty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
    if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) return 0;
    const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
    return st.liquid[i] > 0 ? st.liquidType[i] : 0; // 1水 2岩浆 3蜂蜜 4微光
  }

  // ================= 落叶（Gore_UpdateLeaf :1104） =================

  spawnLeaf(x: number, y: number, gore: number): void {
    if (this.leaves.length >= this.maxLeaves || !LEAF_GORES.has(gore)) return;
    const row = Math.floor(Math.random() * 8);

```

```
        l.rowCounter = 0;
