        if (l.row >= 4 + l.rowGroup * 4) l.row = l.rowGroup * 4;
      }
    }
    this.leaves = this.leaves.filter((l) => !l.dead);
  }

  // ================= 滴水（Gore.cs :383-530 + 碰撞 :955-985） =================

  spawnDrip(x: number, y: number, type: number): void {
    if (this.drips.length >= this.maxDrips || !DRIP_GORES.has(type)) return;
    this.drips.push({ x, y, vy: 0, type, frame: 0, frameCounter: 0, alpha: 0, dead: false });
  }

  private spawnChildFall(d: Drip): void {
    // frame==5 时生成下落子滴（:446-448：NewGore frame=9、速度清零）
    if (this.drips.length >= this.maxDrips) return;
    this.drips.push({ x: d.x, y: d.y, vy: 0, type: d.type, frame: 9, frameCounter: 0, alpha: 0, dead: false });
  }

  updateDrips(worldSurfacePx: number): void {
    const st = this.st;
    if (!st) return;
    for (const d of this.drips) {
      if (d.dead) continue;
      // alpha（:385-398）：943/1160-62 恒 0；地表以下 100；地表 0
      if (d.type === 943 || (d.type >= 1160 && d.type <= 1162)) d.alpha = 0;
      else if (d.y < worldSurfacePx + 8) d.alpha = 0;
      else d.alpha = 100;
      d.frameCounter++;
      let wait = 4;
      if (d.frame <= 4) {
        // 蓄滴（:399-449）：上方非活性格消亡；各帧随机等待
        const tx = Math.floor(d.x / TILE), ty = Math.floor(d.y / TILE) - 1;
        if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && !st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)]) { d.dead = true; continue; }
        if (d.frame <= 2) wait = 24 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
        else if (d.frame === 3) wait = 24 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 96);
        // 原版另有 frame==5 特例(16+Next(64))——在 frame<=4 块内不可达=死代码，不移植
        if (d.type === 716) wait *= 2;
        if (d.type === 717) wait *= 4;
        if ((d.type === 943 || (d.type >= 1160 && d.type <= 1162)) && d.frame < 6) wait = 4;
        if (d.frameCounter >= wait) {
          d.frameCounter = 0; d.frame++;
          if (d.frame === 5) this.spawnChildFall(d);
        }
      } else if (d.frame <= 6) {
        wait = 8;
        if (d.type === 716) wait *= 2;
        if (d.type === 717) wait *= 3;
        if (d.frameCounter >= wait) {
          d.frameCounter = 0; d.frame++;
          if (d.frame === 7) { d.dead = true; continue; }
        }
      } else if (d.frame <= 9) {
        // 下落（:472-508）：6 tick 一帧，重力按水型；clamp [0.5,12]
        wait = 6;
        if (d.type === 716) { wait = Math.floor(wait * 1.5); d.vy += 0.175; }
        else if (d.type === 717) { wait *= 2; d.vy += 0.15; }
        else if (d.type === 943) { wait = Math.floor(wait * 1.5); d.vy += 0.2; }
        else d.vy += 0.2;
        if (d.vy < 0.5) d.vy = 0.5;
        if (d.vy > 12) d.vy = 12;
        if (d.frameCounter >= wait) { d.frameCounter = 0; d.frame++; if (d.frame > 9) d.frame = 7; }
        const preVy = d.vy;
        const r = boxCollide(st, d.x, d.y, 0, d.vy, 14); // TileCollision(position, velocity, 16, 14)
        d.x = r.nx; d.y = r.ny; d.vy = r.nvy;
        if (r.nvy !== preVy) {
          if (d.frame < 10) { d.frame = 10; d.frameCounter = 0; this.dripSound(d, false); } // :968
          continue;
        }
        const liq = this.liquidAt(d.x + 8, d.y + 8);
        if (liq > 0 && d.frame < 10) { d.frame = 10; d.frameCounter = 0; this.dripSound(d, true); } // WetCollision :980
      } else {
        // 溅落 10-14（:510-529）：速度清零、逐帧消散
        wait = 4;
        if (d.type === 716) wait *= 2;
        else if (d.type === 717) wait *= 6;
        d.vy += 0.1;
        if (d.frameCounter >= wait) { d.frameCounter = 0; d.frame++; }
        d.vy = 0;
        if (d.frame > 14) d.dead = true;
      }
    }
    this.drips = this.drips.filter((d) => !d.dead);
  }

  /** 溅落音（Gore.cs :967-984）：flag4 水型（716 蜂蜜/717/943/1160-62）不发声 */
  private dripSound(d: Drip, wet: boolean): void {
    if (d.type === 716 || d.type === 717 || d.type === 943 || (d.type >= 1160 && d.type <= 1162)) return;
    this.onDripSplash?.(d.x + 8, d.y + 8, wet);
  }

  // ================= 绘制 =================

  draw(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: SpriteAtlas): void {
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    // 落叶：32 列(油漆) × 8 行(旋转)，帧 12×14（SpriteFrame(32,8)）
    for (const l of this.leaves) {
      const img = atlas.ensureVImage(`vanilla/Gore_${l.gore}.png`);
      if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) continue;
      const fw = img.naturalWidth / 32, fh = img.naturalHeight / 8;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - l.alpha / 255;
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, l.row * fh, fw, fh, l.x, l.y, fw * l.scale, fh * l.scale);
    }
    // 滴水：18×300 竖条 15 帧（帧高 20）
    for (const d of this.drips) {
      const img = atlas.ensureVImage(`vanilla/Gore_${d.type}.png`);
      if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) continue;
      const fh = img.naturalHeight / 15;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - d.alpha / 255;
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, d.frame * fh, img.naturalWidth, fh, d.x, d.y, img.naturalWidth, fh);
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }
}

export const natureParticles = new NatureParticles();

/** EmitLiquidDrops 的水型映射（TileDrawing :7749-7785）。
 *  waterStyle：12→1147、13/14→706、>1→706+style-1、其余 706；
 *  374→716、375→717、461→943(腐化 1160/猩红 1161/神圣 1162)、709→1383 */
export function dripGoreFor(sheet: number, waterStyle: number, zone: { corrupt?: boolean; crimson?: boolean; hallow?: boolean }): number {
  if (sheet === 374) return 716;
  if (sheet === 375) return 717;
  if (sheet === 461) {
    if (zone.crimson) return 1161;
    if (zone.hallow) return 1162;
    if (zone.corrupt) return 1160;
    return 943;
  }
  if (sheet === 709) return 1383;
  if (waterStyle === 12) return 1147;
  if (waterStyle === 13 || waterStyle === 14) return 706;
  if (waterStyle > 1) return 706 + waterStyle - 1;
  return 706;
}

/** EmitTreeLeaves 的喷出排除表（:7919-7940：-1/912/913/1278 不出叶） */
export function leafEmits(gore: number): boolean {
  return gore !== -1 && gore !== 912 && gore !== 913 && gore !== 1278;

using System.Collections.Generic;

public class AmbientWindSystem
{
	private UnifiedRandom _random = new UnifiedRandom();

	public void Update()
	{

		int num2 = tileWorkSpace.Y + tileWorkSpace.Height;

		{
			for (int j = tileWorkSpace.Y; j < num2; j++)

	}

  - TestAirCloud(x,y): 1/120000 → check ±2 cross tiles all "allow wind" → add to _spotsForAirboneWind.
  - floor cloud: if tile active, slope 0, not halfBrick, tileSolid[type]; and tile above (y-1) NOT solid → 1/120 chance: SpawnFloorCloud(x, y); 1/3 → another at (x, y-1).

**SpawnFloorCloud**: position = (x*16+8, (y-1)*16+8) world center-ish (ToWorldCoordinates of Point(x, y-1) gives tile top-left +8,+8). num = 16 * randFloat; position.Y -= num; if num < 4 → type = 1090 else type = rand 1087..1089. velocity = UnitX * 0.4 * WindForVisuals (wind direction/speed). scale = 0.8 + rand*0.2.

	private void UpdateAmbientFloorCloud()
	{
		timeLeft -= GoreID.Sets.DisappearSpeed[type];
		if (timeLeft <= 0)
		{
			active = false;
			return;
		}
		bool flag = false;
		Point point = (position + new Vector2(15f, 0f)).ToTileCoordinates();
		Tile tile = Main.tile[point.X, point.Y];
		Tile tile2 = Main.tile[point.X, point.Y + 1];
		Tile tile3 = Main.tile[point.X, point.Y + 2];
		if (tile == null || tile2 == null || tile3 == null)
		{
			active = false;
			return;
		}
		if (WorldGen.SolidTile(tile) || (!WorldGen.SolidTile(tile2) && !WorldGen.SolidTile(tile3)))
		{
			flag = true;
		}
		if (timeLeft <= 30)
		{
			flag = true;
		}
		velocity.X = 0.4f * Main.WindForVisuals;
		if (!flag)
		{
			if (alpha > 220)
			{
				alpha--;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			alpha++;
			if (alpha >= 255)
			{
				active = false;
				return;
			}
		}
		position += velocity;
	}

	private void UpdateAmbientAirborneCloud()

		if (tile == null)
		{
			active = false;
			return;
		}
		if (WorldGen.SolidTile(tile))
		{
			flag = true;
		}
		if (timeLeft <= 60)
		{
			flag = true;

x < x0 + 120; x++) {\n      for (let y = y0; y < y0 + 30; y++) {\n        this.trySpawningWind(x, y);\n      }\n    }\n    if (this.windCounter % 30 === 0) {                      // :46-49\n      for (const s of this.airSpots) this.spawnAirborneCloud(s.x, s.y, wind);\n      this.airSpots.length = 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** AmbientWindSystem.TrySpawningWind（:69-93） */\n  private trySpawningWind(x: number, y: number): void {\n    const st = this.st!;\n    if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || x < 10 || y < 10 || x >= st.w - 10 || y >= st.h - 10) return; d <= 2; d++) {\n      if (d === 0) continue;\n      if (!this.tileAllowsWind(x + d, y) || !this.tileAllowsWind(x, y + d)) return;\n    }\n    this.airSpots.push({ x, y });\n  }\n\n  /** DoesTileAllowWind（:168-172）：空气/非实心格允许风 */\n  private tileAllowsWind(x: number, y: number): boolean {\n    const st = this.st!;\n    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;\n    return st.type[st.idx(x, y)] === 0 || !st.isSolid(x, y);\n  }\n\n  /** SpawnFloorCloud（:121-133）：(x,y-1) 格中心出生，上浮 0-16px；\n   *  上浮 <4px 换贴地皮型 1090，否则 1087-1089；vx = 0.4×视觉风 */\n  private spawnFloorCloud(x: number, y: number): void {\n    if (this.clouds.length >= this.maxClouds) return;\n    const lift = 16 * Math.random();\n    const type = lift < 4 ? 1090 : 1087 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);\n    this.clouds.push({\n      x: x * TILE + 8, y: (y - 1) * TILE + 8 - lift,\n      vx: 0, vy: 0, alpha: 0, timeLeft: 600,          // NewGore 初始（Gore.cs:1347-1352）\n      scale: 0.8 + Math.random() * 0.2, type, rotation: 0, airborne: false, dead: false,\n    });\n  }\n\n  /** SpawnAirborneCloud（:80-118）：沿缓转向航向排 2-5（1/4 时 9-15）朵云链，\n   *  贴型 1091/1093 交替，尺度 1.1+rand×2.2（变体 ×1.2/×0.2） */\n  private spawnAirborneCloud(x: number, y: number, wind: number): void {\n    let n = 2 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 4);        // Next(2,6)\n    let baseScale = 1.1, varScale = 2.2;\n    if (Math.random() < 0.25) { n = 9 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7); baseScale = 1.1; varScale = 1.2; }\n    else if (Math.random() < 0.25) { n = 9 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7); baseScale = 1.1; varScale = 0.2; }\n    const TURN = 0.023561945;\n    let turn = TURN * (Math.random() * 2 - 1);\n    let step = TURN * (Math.random() * 2 - 1);\n    while (step > -0.011780973 && step < 0.011780973) {\n      step = TURN * (Math.random() * 2 - 1);\n    }\n    turn -= step * n * 0.5;                            // :89\n    let px = x * TILE + 8, py = y * TILE + 8;\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n      if (Math.random() < 0.1) step *= Math.random() * 2 - 1;   // :94-97\n      const jx = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 4, jy = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 4;  // NextVector2Circular(4,4)\n      const type = 1091 + (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 0 : 2);        // 1091 + Next(2)*2\n      const scale = baseScale + Math.random() * varScale;\n      const ang = turn + step;\n      const dx = Math.cos(ang) * 1.4, dy = Math.sin(ang) * 1.4; // UnitX.RotatedBy(num9)*1.4\n      if (this.clouds.length < this.maxClouds) {\n        this.clouds.push({\n          x: px + jx - 10, y: py + jy,                // vector2 + vector3 - (10,0)\n          vx: dx * wind, vy: dy * wind,               // vector4 * WindForVisuals\n          alpha: 0, timeLeft: 600, scale, type, rotation: ang, airborne: true, dead: false,\n        });\n      }\n      px += dx * 6.5 * scale;                          // vector2 += vector4 * 6.5 * num8\n      turn = ang;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** UpdateAmbientFloorCloud（Gore.cs:111-148）：贴地漂移；头部格实心（钻墙）或\n   *  下方两格全空（悬空）或 timeLeft≤30 → 淡出至 255 消散 */\n  private stepFloorCloud(c: Cloud, wind: number): void {\n    c.timeLeft -= 1;                                   // DisappearSpeed 默认 1\n    if (c.timeLeft <= 0) { c.dead = true; return; }\n    const st = this.st!;\n    const hx = Math.floor((c.x + 15) / TILE), hy = Math.floor(c.y / TILE);\n    if (!st.inBounds(hx, hy) || hy + 2 >= st.h) { c.dead = true; return; }\n    let flag = st.isSolid(hx, hy) || (!st.isSolid(hx, hy + 1) && !st.isSolid(hx, hy + 2));\n    if (c.timeLeft <= 30) flag = true;\n    c.vx = 0.4 * wind;\n    if (!flag) { if (c.alpha > 220) c.alpha--; }\n    else {\n      c.alpha++;\n      if (c.alpha >= 255) { c.dead = true; return; }\n    }\n    c.x += c.vx;\n  }\n\n  /** UpdateAmbientAirborneCloud（Gore.cs:157-202）：航向 = 速度角；头部格实心或\n   *  timeLeft≤60 → 1/5 概率逐帧淡出 */\n  private stepAirborneCloud(c: Cloud): void {\n    c.timeLeft -= 1;\n    if (c.timeLeft <= 0) { c.dead = true; return; }\n    const st = this.st!;\n    const hx = Math.floor((c.x + 15) / TILE), hy = Math.floor(c.y / TILE);\n    if (!st.inBounds(hx, hy)) { c.dead = true; return; }\n    c.rotation = Math.atan2(c.vy, c.vx);\n    let flag = st.isSolid(hx, hy);\n    if (c.timeLeft <= 60) flag = true;\n    if (!flag) { if (c.alpha > 240 && Math.random() < 0.2) c.alpha--; }\n    else {\n      if (Math.random() < 0.2) c.alpha++;\n      if (c.alpha >= 255) { c.dead = true; return; }\n    }\n    c.x += c.vx;\n    c.y += c.vy;\n  }"
}
